- mechanism: The focus already makes ḍuḥā a grazing and feeding interval. A named she-camel and her specific watering share convert that creaturely use into an apportioned commons whose turn must be guarded rather than seized or withheld.
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B006: Protective guarding and withholding supply the focus-side tension between preserving a share and monopolizing it.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B003: Early grazing supplies the creaturely time-slot that can be treated as an allotted use.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The concrete she-camel supplies the living claimant whose access makes the allocation testable.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of watering and its channel supply the explicit resource-allocation mechanism.

## ethic of pace
- reading: Ḍuḥā's gradual arrival models a disciplined pace of disclosure, sharply opposed to overflow and impulsive mobilization.
- mechanism: Overflow and impulsive collective motion contrast with messenger-side gentleness and deliberation. These context velocities activate the focus branch of slow ḍuḥā: the difference between ordered manifestation and ruin may lie partly in pace, not only destination.
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B006: Gentle delay supplies the focus-side norm of unhurried emergence.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Overflowing force supplies acceleration beyond a containing limit.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: A group's rushing departure supplies impulsive motion into action.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B004: Gentleness and deliberation supply the counter-velocity aligned with measured ḍuḥā.

## leveling double edge
- reading: Full exposure has a double edge: it can complete and equalize a field for sight, but imagined without limit it can also flatten distinction into erasure.
- mechanism: The same forming verb spans completion of the self and equalization after extirpation. Against ḍuḥā's unhidden surface, this repetition activates a double-edged leveling model: exposure may complete a field by making it even and legible, yet total leveling can erase the lives and differences it exposed.
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Open outward prominence supplies the relatively unshadowed surface on which leveling becomes visible.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion supply leveling's constructive pole in the formed self.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the destructive operation adjoining the second leveling.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality between things supplies the flattening pole capable of removing differentiation.

## afterlight consequence
- reading: The sun's ḍuḥā models an event with a tail: what becomes manifest enters succession and persists as consequence, trace, or remainder.
- mechanism: The surah's context opens with a follower and closes with an aftermath. A remainder that follows and an end that leaves a residue turn ḍuḥā's extension into a causal tail: manifestation continues in what comes after it, whether or not that aftermath is feared.
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B001: Extended forenoon supplies duration capable of projecting beyond the immediate bright moment.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B003: A remainder following what came first supplies the initial image of residual continuation.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fear as expectation of harm supplies the prospective stance toward what an action may leave behind.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The end and consequence of a thing supply the terminal phase of the causal sequence.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A surviving remainder or trace supplies the residue through which the bright interval continues after itself.

## sacrificial brightness
- reading: At the edge of the lexical field, the bright interval carries a sacrificial resonance that makes violence against the protected animal feel like a desecration of an ordered life-giving turn.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B004: The Adha sacrificial animal supplies the focus-root ritual resonance.
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B002: The divine name used in oath or call supplies a formal bridge from the opening oath to the protected-animal warning.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The she-camel supplies the concrete animal around which the ritual resonance can activate.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Hamstringing an animal supplies the violent act that converts sacrifice-like imagery into profanation rather than sanctioned rite.

## oath necklace
- reading: The focus pair is the first pendant in a linked oath-necklace whose successive images gain force through their ordered attachment.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B004: Necklace pendants supply the focus-root image of a discrete luminous item hung in a series.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following in sequence supplies the linkage by which one oath-image hangs after another.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B010: The secondary mapped necklace image supplies a distant recurrence of the artifact near the narrative turn.

## aperture sky
- reading: At the exploratory material edge, ḍuḥā is disclosure channeled through an aperture in a built field, with visibility shaped by what admits it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: Solar light supplies the beam whose mode of entry is being reimagined.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Outward appearance supplies the visible result produced by admitted light.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: A narrow opening through which something enters supplies the form-distant aperture mechanism.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Assembly by joining parts supplies the constructed boundary around the aperture.

## broken succession
- reading: The recurring solar interval becomes the standard against which violent social action appears as a severing of succession—an act that leaves no follower.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B001: Extended daylight supplies the recurring interval capable of carrying succession.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Explicit following supplies the normal continuity established immediately after the focus.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B005: A final item after which nothing follows supplies the image of succession cut off.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the terminal force that makes the break in continuation total.


# Focus 91:2

## ordered celestial succession
- reading: An oath by the moon specifically in the condition of taking an ordered place after a feminine antecedent.
- mechanism: The moon and its light are placed in bodily, ranked, or temporal sequence after an antecedent that remains unresolved within the focus ayah alone.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The celestial body together with its illumination supplies the visible entity whose position is tracked.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Physical, ranked, or sequential following supplies the ordered relation to the unresolved feminine referent.

## dependent afterlight
- reading: The moon can be heard as an afterlight or remainder that carries forward what preceded it, making dependence coexist with sequence.
- mechanism: What follows can be the residue of what came before, so the moon is not merely a second coequal object but a dependent afterlight carrying a prior presence forward.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon's visible illumination provides the material that can persist as an afterlight.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B003: A remainder coming after an earlier portion turns succession into residual continuation rather than mere adjacency.

## antiphonal response
- reading: The moon answers a prior presence like a second voice, preserving responsive relay alongside physical succession.
- mechanism: Following can be antiphonal: a second voice answers a first. Crossed into the visual domain, the lunar presence becomes a response to an unresolved prior signal rather than a silent trailer.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The sky-visible moon and its light function as the second signal in the proposed visual relay.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B007: A voice answering a preceding voice supplies the responsive, antiphonal structure assigned to lunar following.

## attached claim
- reading: Following can also be an attached due or turn that remains bound to the antecedent.
- mechanism: Following may describe an encumbrance or due that accompanies its source. The moon's relation to the feminine object can therefore be tested as an attached turn or claim, not only a location behind it.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The recognizable lunar bearer gives the proposed attached claim a recurring visible locus.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B004: A due or liability that follows its possessor supplies the non-spatial bond between follower and antecedent.

## solar radiance handoff
- reading: The moon occupies the wake of the sun and its manifested brightness, functioning as successor rather than isolated companion.
- mechanism: The immediately preceding sun and its extended, exposed brightness resolve the feminine object and turn generic lunar succession into a handoff from solar manifestation to lunar presence.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its illumination provide the successor that receives the handoff.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Ordered following provides the transfer relation from the preceding solar clause.
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: The solar body and its radiance supply the most immediate feminine antecedent and the first member of the handoff.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B001: Brightness extending through the day gives the antecedent a temporal wake into which the moon can follow.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Exposure to the sun and emergence into visibility make the first phase an active manifestation rather than a mere named object.

## reversible visibility circuit
- reading: Lunar following participates in a repeating circuit in which visibility is handed on, disclosed, and covered again.
- mechanism: Day opens and discloses while night covers; placed between them, lunar following becomes one transition in a reversible visibility circuit rather than the endpoint of a one-way chase.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: Lunar illumination supplies the visible state whose place within alternating exposure and cover is being modeled.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Sequential following connects the focus to the next state changes without forcing a terminal stopping point.
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: Day opening through light supplies an expansive visibility phase after the focus.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance supply the circuit's disclosure operation.
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night and its darkness provide the opposed temporal regime.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering laid over something supplies the inverse operation to disclosure and closes the alternating circuit.

## constructed upper track
- reading: The moon's succession is an ordered placement or traversal within a built upper layer over an extended lower field.
- mechanism: A raised upper field assembled by joining parts is opposed to a lower field spread out beneath it. Lunar following can therefore be spatialized as ordered traversal within a constructed, layered geometry.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The sky-visible lunar body supplies the moving component located in the proposed upper layer.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Bodily or ranked succession supplies ordered placement along the constructed field.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The over-arching upper expanse supplies the layer in which lunar movement is situated.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joining parts into a construction turns the upper field from empty backdrop into ordered architecture.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower realm opposed to the sky supplies the second layer and fixes the vertical contrast.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending provide the broad lower plane against which the upper traversal is legible.

## phase maturation
- reading: The moon's following can include its own ordered advance through visible degrees toward fullness.
- mechanism: The lunar branch scope admits different points from crescent to fullness, while later completion and growth branches activate following as progression through degrees of maturation rather than movement by one fixed body behind another.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The lunar scope includes its visible light and naming across emergence and fullness, supplying a body capable of phased maturation.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following by sequence or rank supplies the ordered degrees through which the lunar state can advance.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion activate fullness as the direction of the proposed phase sequence.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Increase and growth supply the developmental motion between successive degrees.

## falsifiable signal relay
- reading: The moon becomes a model of faithful signal uptake: a second public response that contrasts with speech being falsified or blocked.
- mechanism: The antiphonal baseline becomes a social signal chain: speech is emitted and carried, can stir a receiver into motion, and can be rejected by attributing falsehood. Lunar following now models faithful relay in contrast with interrupted or falsified uptake.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The visible lunar signal supplies the second, publicly legible member of the relay.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B007: A responding voice following another supplies the relay structure that the later speech sequence activates.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing falsehood to a source supplies the mechanism by which a received signal is refused rather than carried forward.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Stirring something from rest supplies the transition from reception to activated response.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Audible production supplies the first voiced signal in the social relay.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: A messenger and message supply the carrier relation between source, utterance, and receiver.

## lunar resource turn
- reading: The moon's following can cue a recurring camel-provisioning and water-allocation turn whose due status travels with its beneficiary.
- mechanism: Focus branches connect moonlight with abundant water and pasture, delayed camel feeding, and an attached due; the later camel and its allotted irrigation activate a practical reading in which celestial succession cues a recurring husbandry entitlement.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B008: Abundant water and grazing associated with this root supply the ecological resource at stake.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B010: Delayed evening provisioning of camels makes lunar time materially relevant to herd care.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B004: A right or liability that follows its holder supplies the recurring entitlement attached to the schedule.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The camel image supplies the concrete herd beneficiary whose provision must recur.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An allotted share of watering and its channel supply the due turn governed by the proposed schedule.

## sequence cutoff reversal
- reading: The clause foregrounds continuity itself, while also admitting that a successor can leave a prior companion behind and that sequences can be violently cut off.
- mechanism: The focus establishes continuation, while later branches introduce a last item with no successor, complete eradication, and omission. Following is thereby sharpened as continuity precisely because the context imagines operations that terminate a sequence.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The recurring visible lunar successor supplies the positive instance of continued sequence.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Ordered continuation supplies the relation later terminal branches negate.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B005: Leaving a companion behind after prior following introduces the possibility that succession also supersedes.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B005: A final item after which nothing follows supplies the exact negative boundary of the focus relation.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Total eradication supplies a force capable of imposing terminal closure.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B011: Dropping or omitting supplies the final operation by which a member can be removed from sequence.

## consequence as tail
- reading: The moon's following becomes a visible grammar of aftermath: an originating act or presence carries a tail, claim, and eventual consequence.
- mechanism: A remainder and attached claim in the focus are joined by later images of a tail, anticipated harm, outcome, and punishment after an offense. Following becomes causally loaded aftermath: what occurs leaves something attached behind it that arrives as consequence.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The visible lunar successor gives the abstract aftermath model a recurring perceptible form.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B003: A remainder coming after what preceded supplies the residue left by an initiating event.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B004: A due that follows its holder supplies the attachment binding aftermath to its source.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The tail or final part of a thing supplies a concrete image for consequence trailing an act.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Alarm that anticipates harm supplies prospective awareness of what may follow.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The final outcome of a thing supplies the endpoint toward which the causal sequence runs.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B007: Penalty arriving after wrongdoing supplies an explicitly accountable form of delayed consequence.

## glare that conceals
- reading: The successor can disclose and overwhelm at once, so following light may culminate in cognitive cover rather than clarity.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B005: Vision confused by intense whiteness supplies the paradoxical capacity of the luminous follower to impair seeing.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Ordinary succession lets perceptual overload arrive as a later state rather than replacing the focus relation.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Disclosure supplies the expected clarifying pole against which the reversal becomes visible.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B007: A covering over understanding supplies cognitive concealment rather than merely physical darkness.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Reason and inner apprehension locate the reveal-or-overwhelm effect within perception.

## successor as overthrow
- reading: At the exploratory edge, the follower's arrival can be imagined as casting down and replacing the preceding regime.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon remains the arriving visible entity, preventing the reversal from becoming an independent destruction theme.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B003: Throwing someone down supplies the exploratory reversal in which arrival displaces rather than merely trails a predecessor.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Disabling an animal's legs supplies a concrete loss of support under the proposed overthrow.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Complete eradication magnifies displacement into regime-ending force.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B011: Dropping or omitting supplies the flattening result assigned to the successor's arrival.

## lunar wager over a due turn
- reading: At the exploratory edge, succession becomes a wager over a due turn, exposing how deceit and excess can capture an otherwise ordered share.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B007: Wagering, contest, and deceptive victory supply a model in which succession is a turn that can be captured unfairly.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B004: A right that follows its holder supplies the due turn over which the contest occurs.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Assigning falsehood supplies the deceptive move by which another party's claim can be discredited.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing a limit in disobedience supplies the rule-breaking that converts a turn into domination.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A defined watering share supplies the scarce entitlement whose sequence can be honored or seized.


# Focus 91:3

## manifesting day
- reading: Day acts upon an unresolved feminine object and brings her from concealment into manifest visibility.
- mechanism: Day is an active visibility-making interval: its opening in light supplies the medium, and the verb moves an unresolved feminine object from hiddenness into manifestation.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: The day opening in light supplies the temporal and luminous field of disclosure.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Emergence after hiddenness gives the verb its object-changing action.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B007: Spreading daylight that removes darkness makes the named agent and its revealing action mutually reinforcing.

## channelled opening
- reading: Day carves and widens a corridor of visibility through what had enclosed the object.
- mechanism: Daylight behaves materially: it cuts, widens, and opens a channel through an occluded field, so revelation is spatial clearance rather than brightness merely laid on top.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B001: A flowing channel cutting ground supplies the material geometry of daylight advancing through obstruction.
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Widening until flow or open space appears turns day into an expanding visual aperture.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering supplies the result produced by the channel-like opening.

## polished perception
- reading: Day clarifies the very conditions of seeing, polishing perception until she can be discerned.
- mechanism: The action can operate on the medium or faculty of seeing: daylight burnishes perception until the object resolves, rather than only throwing light onto an unchanged observer.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: The light-opened day supplies the condition under which visual clarification can work.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B002: Polishing a surface and clearing eyesight recasts disclosure as refinement of the perceiving interface.

## presentational unveiling
- reading: Day presents her into visibility as an event of display, with an implied beholding position.
- mechanism: The object is not merely exposed by ambient light; she is staged and presented before a gaze, giving the clause a relational and ceremonial contour.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: The opening of day provides the public interval in which presentation becomes possible.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B003: Deliberate bridal unveiling supplies a staged relation among agent, feminine object, and implied beholder.

## displacing clearance
- reading: Day clears by displacement, forcing concealment out of the field so the object stands exposed.
- mechanism: Revelation may be subtractive: day makes room by driving away what occupies or conceals the field, so clarity has a cost of displacement.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening supply the cleared space into which visibility expands.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B004: Departure or expulsion supplies the subtractive means by which the field becomes clear.

## solar referent
- reading: Day actively brings the sun or her radiant face into view, with the broader world still possible as a secondary object.
- mechanism: The immediately preceding solar and midmorning cues give the feminine object a concrete luminous candidate: day manifests the sun or her radiant face rather than an unspecified scene.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Manifestation after hiddenness remains the focus action whose object is being identified.
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: The sun and its radiance supply the nearest feminine luminous entity for the focus suffix.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight and appearance make the preceding clause itself a disclosure event.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B005: Clear midmorning radiance sharpens the luminous identity of what day reveals.

## processional phase
- reading: Day's revealing is a phase-transition within a procession of luminous and obscuring agents.
- mechanism: Moonlight and following turn the focus action into one phase of an ordered procession: manifestation is achieved through succession and replacement, not by an isolated burst of light.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: The bounded daylight interval supplies one distinguishable phase in the procession.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B007: Daylight spreading against darkness gives the phase its revealing effect.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its sky-light supply a neighboring, distinct mode of illumination.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and serial continuation supply the temporal arrow that links luminous phases.

## covering reversal
- reading: Day peels back a night-laid covering, so visibility is a reversible contest between disclosure and enclosure.
- mechanism: The following night clause supplies the exact inverse operation. Day's disclosure is therefore the active reversal of a cover laid over the same object.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering after hiddenness names the focus-side reversal.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B007: Daylight spreading so darkness recedes gives the reversal a physical medium.
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night as the dark contrary of day supplies the alternating agent.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A cover rising over and concealing something supplies the layer that day removes.

## worldmaking clearance
- reading: Daylight is a constructive act that opens, extends, and completes the world's field of appearance.
- mechanism: Building, spreading, and bringing a being to integral form recast daylight as one constructive operation in world-making: it opens and finishes a habitable perceptual field.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Widening into open space makes the focus operation constructive and spatial.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Manifestation supplies the perceptual completion produced by that opening.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joining parts into a built whole supplies an architectural analogue for making visibility.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Extension and spreading supply the horizontal enlargement of the revealed field.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion supply the finishing condition of a world made fully perceptible.

## marked legibility
- reading: Day makes her condition identifiable and publicly readable, converting appearance into a sign.
- mechanism: A non-dominant mapped inventory for the sky root activates visible marking and diagnosis by an observer. Day does more than expose a shape: it marks the object into public legibility.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B006: Open notoriety turns manifestation into being unmistakably known.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: A visible mark that makes its bearer recognizable supplies the shift from visibility to identification.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: An observer reading condition from a sign supplies the diagnostic function of daylight.

## breathing dawn
- reading: The world seems to exhale day, opening from nocturnal enclosure into a living field of visibility.
- mechanism: The self root's dawn-like opening and the rupture root's daybreak image animate the focus: day is an exhalation or release by which the closed world breathes into visibility.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening until release supplies the expanding motion of the breath image.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B007: Daylight spreading out of darkness supplies the visible result of release.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B009: The opening of dawn figured like breath directly couples respiration and daybreak.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B002: Daybreak splitting out of night supplies the release boundary.

## breach and moral exposure
- reading: Day becomes a model of exposure in which what a self protects, purifies, or buries can be brought into the open.
- mechanism: Wide rupture, torn covering, protection, purification, and concealment turn optical revelation into an analogue of moral exposure: daylight makes buried alternatives available without itself choosing between them.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering supplies the focus anchor for bringing concealed material into appearance.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A wide split and release make disclosure forceful rather than merely incremental.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Deviation figured as tearing a veil lets moral breach and optical breach coexist.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Protection from harm supplies the counterforce and the possible cost of exposure.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness supply one possible state that disclosure can make evident.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Insertion into hiddenness supplies the direct inverse of the focus unveiling.

## cultivating incision
- reading: Day cuts open and irrigates a perceptual field, making growth and self-cultivation imaginable.
- mechanism: The river-like day, the plough's cut, growth, and nurturing completion form an ecological mechanism: illumination incises an occluded surface so that a viable field can develop.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B001: A watercourse cutting ground supplies both flow and incision.
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Widening into open space converts the cut into a usable field.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving and cutting sharpen the material action latent in the focus.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: A cultivator splitting earth turns the opening into productive preparation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the result enabled by the opened field.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and bringing to completion supply sustained cultivation beyond the first cut.

## floodlight overflow
- reading: Day inundates the field with a powerful flow of visibility, at once potentially excessive and channelled into shares.
- mechanism: Overflowing water activates the day root's river branch, while an allotted watering-channel image gives that force a course. Revelation becomes a flood of light that can exceed limits yet still travel through apportioned channels.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B001: Abundant flowing water and a ground-cutting channel provide the focus-side hydraulic substrate.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B007: Daylight spreading until darkness is removed converts water flow into a flood of visibility.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Water rising past its bound supplies the force and excess of the disclosure.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: The non-dominant mapped branch extends water-overflow into analogous kinds of excess rather than leaving it purely literal.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An allotted share of watering and its channel constrain the flow into a distributive course.

## visibility not veracity
- reading: Day makes her available as evidence, but display, speech, and the observer can still distort what the appearance means.
- mechanism: A display can misrepresent its condition, and speech can operate as conjecture. These cues weaken any equation of being manifest with being true: daylight produces evidence, not infallible interpretation.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B003: Staged presentation supplies the focus-side possibility that appearance is curated.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B006: Public notoriety distinguishes being widely visible from being correctly understood.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B009: A garment whose appearance misstates its condition supplies a concrete false-display mechanism.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B011: Speech functioning as conjecture supplies interpretive uncertainty after manifestation.

## roused world
- reading: Day summons latent forms into visible activity, making revelation also an awakening.
- mechanism: Stirring what was motionless turns disclosure into activation: day does not only reveal a waiting world but rouses its forms from latency into visible activity.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: The opening daylight interval supplies the activating condition.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Emergence from hiddenness supplies the transition from latency to appearance.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing the still from rest supplies the kinetic arrow added to visual emergence.

## luminous message
- reading: By unveiling her, day makes a nonverbal declaration whose visible object functions as testimony.
- mechanism: A thing's capacity to indicate and the arrival of a messenger recast daylight as nonverbal testimony. Its unveiling communicates a claim before any spoken warning occurs.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B006: Open, unmistakable notoriety makes the revealed object publicly communicative.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's own indication functioning as speech supplies nonverbal signification.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply the communicative function assigned to the daylight sign.

## destructive clearance
- reading: Day's unveiling can be imagined as a forceful clearance that cuts an aperture and displaces whatever maintained concealment.
- mechanism: Wounding, opening a gap, eradication, and equalization strengthen the subtractive baseline: making a field clear can mean cutting through it, emptying it, and leveling what resisted.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Opening and widening provide the focus-side cleared field.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B004: Expulsion and departure make clearance subtractive rather than merely illuminative.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B001: Wounding and overcoming supply the violent edge of cutting a field open.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B014: A gap between two things supplies the aperture left by the cut.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the limiting case in which clearing removes the field's occupants.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization supplies the flattened end-state after destructive clearance.

## aftereffect of disclosure
- reading: Disclosure leaves a trace, entails an outcome, and recurs within a sequence whose next phase can cover the object again.
- mechanism: Tail, end, consequence, succession, and return make the temporal clause consequential. An unveiling leaves a remainder and enters a cycle; it is not exhausted in the instant of seeing.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: A bounded daylight period gives disclosure a temporal edge and therefore an after.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Manifestation supplies the event whose residue and consequence are being tracked.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The tail or end of a thing supplies a trailing remainder after the event.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Succession supplies the replacement of one phase by another.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: Outcome supplies consequence rather than mere temporal following.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B009: Return again and again makes disclosure recurrent rather than final.

## day as rebuke
- reading: Her exposure itself acts like a harsh public rebuke: daylight answers denial by forcing what was hidden into view.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B004: Harsh verbal driving-back personifies day as an admonishing agent.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Public uncovering gives the rebuke its nonverbal act: what was denied is made visible.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies the human stance to which visible exposure can answer.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Uttered speech supplies the explicit mode whose function daylight performs without words.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Message-bearing turns the exposing day into an analogue of communicated warning.

## nuptial display
- reading: The clause can faintly stage a ceremonial unveiling in which day presents her into a relational field of beholding.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: The opening public interval of day supplies the setting for display.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B003: Deliberate bridal presentation directly supplies the ceremonial unveiling mechanism.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B006: The construction root's marital-entry branch activates a nuptial relation beyond generic architecture.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B004: The covering root's sexual-union branch supplies an intimate counter-image to daytime presentation.

## predatory gaze
- reading: Day can be carried as a searching optic that scans, finds, and exposes her as though locating quarry.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B008: A lifted searching gaze supplies the focus-side scanning action.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B003: Pursuit or hunting under moonlight turns illumination into a condition for locating a target.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B006: Going out to hunt supplies an active seeking frame around the revealed object.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B013: The bird of prey supplies the elevated searching perspective latent in the focus gaze branch.

## burnished blade
- reading: Day burnishes a cutting edge of perception, sharply separating forms and exposing the violence possible in clear distinctions.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B002: Polishing a blade and clearing vision fuse optical clarity with a sharpened edge.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving and cutting supply the act performed by the sharpened distinction.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B001: Wounding and overcoming preserve the dangerous edge of making a separation clear.

## day as cleft
- reading: Day is the recurrent cleft opened in night, an interval through which she becomes visible.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B003: Widening until an open space appears supplies the geometry of a cleft.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Manifestation supplies what becomes possible through the aperture.
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's contrary darkness supplies the enclosing material on both sides of the interval.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A broad rupture and release supply the event that opens the interval.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B014: A gap between two things stabilizes the day as interval rather than luminous substance.

## stealth snatch
- reading: Day can be imagined as suddenly snatching away the cover on which a deceptive appearance depended.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B006: A sudden stealthy snatch recasts daylight as abruptly taking concealment away.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: The resulting manifestation identifies concealment, rather than the object, as what was snatched.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B007: Mastery through gambling or deception supplies the risky, non-gentle manner of taking.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B009: False display supplies the concealment that the sudden daylight event strips away.

## embodied fear
- reading: Daylight can also figure the moment an inward condition writes itself visibly onto a living bearer.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Manifestation after hiddenness supplies the transition from inward affect to outward sign.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B006: Open notoriety makes the once-private condition publicly readable.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The mind and inward apprehension supply the interior whose condition may surface.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B005: Fear visibly appearing on a person supplies the embodied disclosure mechanism.


# Focus 91:4

## surface veil
- reading: Night is an agentive veil that advances over and withholds a feminine field from view.
- mechanism: Night's darkness behaves as a moving upper layer: when its phase arrives, it overlays the pronominal field and restricts access to it without yet implying damage or permanence.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night as the dark opposite of day supplies the temporal-dark layer and serves as the covering agent.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A veil laid over and concealing something supplies the contact geometry and makes the verb an external overlay on -hā.

## arriving night
- reading: Night itself comes upon the object like a recurring visitor whose arrival effects the cover.
- mechanism: The clause can stage an encounter rather than a static screen: night enters, comes upon, or visits the pronominal object as an event with approach and contact.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B002: Entering or undertaking something by night turns the temporal noun into an enacted phase rather than a passive background.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B003: Coming to or frequenting a target supplies the approach relation and lets night arrive upon -hā.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B005: The non-dominant mapped inventory independently preserves visiting or habitual arrival and keeps the encounter reading live.

## total overtaking
- reading: Night overtakes the entire referential field as an encompassing event.
- mechanism: The scale of covering enlarges from a surface veil to a total event: night takes the whole field, temporarily leaving no exterior vantage within it.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's darkness supplies the medium capable of filling the field.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B002: An enveloping event that overtakes people supplies total scope and raises covering from placement to seizure.

## reversible celestial relay
- reading: Night's veil is one reversible turn in a patterned handoff between disclosure and concealment.
- mechanism: Solar exposure, lunar following, daylight opening, disclosure, and nocturnal covering form a relay of contrary operations over a recurring feminine field. Covering is therefore a phase-change that answers disclosure and remains answerable by it.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night supplies the dark phase that takes its place opposite the exposed phase.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: Veiling supplies the closing operation in the relay.
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: The sun and its radiance supply the primary exposing pole against which night operates.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B005: Clear forenoon light supplies the state of maximal visibility that covering reverses.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon's light supplies a second luminous phase inside the larger alternation.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following in sequence supplies the ordered handoff from one phase to another.
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: Day opening through light supplies an expanding aperture that opposes nocturnal closure.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Disclosure and appearance supply the exact inverse operation to veiling.

## recurrent visitation
- reading: Night repeatedly visits -hā in its appointed place within a returning procession.
- mechanism: Following, succession, and repeated return strengthen the focus-only encounter model: night does not merely exist around the object but arrives in its turn, withdraws, and comes again.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B002: Entry into nocturnal action supplies the enacted arrival of the phase.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B003: Coming upon a target supplies night with the motion of a visitor.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B005: The split-root visiting branch preserves recurrence and prevents the reading from collapsing back into mere opacity.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and serial arrival supply the procession in which night can take a turn.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Succession and replacement supply the recurrent after-one-another structure.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B009: Returning again and again supplies periodicity to the visitor model.

## temporary canopy
- reading: Night is a temporary cosmic canopy constructed across an extended lower field.
- mechanism: An overhead expanse, construction by joined parts, the lower ground, and its spreading convert the veil into architecture. Night becomes a temporary roof or sheath laid across the extended field below.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night's darkness supplies the material quality of the temporary roof.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A cover resting over something supplies the roof-to-field relation.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: What rises above and shades supplies the overhead span that night can occupy.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Building by joining parts supplies the constructive assembly of an enclosing layer.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower counterpart to the sky supplies the surface over which the canopy extends.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supplies the widening footprint of the night-cover.

## consciousness eclipse
- reading: Night can eclipse the inward self's access to understanding while leaving its formed integrity underneath.
- mechanism: The affected field can now be modeled as the inward capacity of a formed living self. Night covers understanding in a reversible eclipse: access is suspended while the self remains intact.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night supplies the recurring environmental phase under which awareness can be eclipsed.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B005: Being overcome so that consciousness and understanding are covered supplies the internal effect.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B007: The non-dominant mapped branch independently preserves loss of understanding and keeps the perceptual reading distinct from an outer veil.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The reason and inward awareness of the self supply the faculty being covered.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Completion and integral formation preserve the self beneath the temporary eclipse.

## protective enclosure
- reading: Night's cover can be a defensive withdrawal that preserves the interior against breach and permits growth.
- mechanism: A cover can intercept a breach instead of merely causing blindness. Read beside rupture, self-protection, and growth, night becomes a guarded enclosure whose opacity may preserve an interior long enough for it to develop.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night supplies the dark interval in which an interior can be withdrawn from exposure.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: The veil supplies a boundary capable of intercepting as well as obscuring.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Deviation as piercing a cover supplies the invasive breach against which enclosure can function.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Repelling harm by a protective barrier supplies the defensive function of covering.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Placing the self within protection supplies an interior that the night-cover can shelter.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the process that protected withdrawal may enable.

## soil like incubation
- reading: Night may cover like soil: the hidden phase can hold a process that later splits its enclosure and grows.
- mechanism: The spread fertile ground, its plowing, hidden insertion, subsequent increase, and eventual fissure activate a seed-like material analogy. Night covering -hā can be incubation under a dark layer: concealment holds latent emergence rather than negating it.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night supplies the dark interval analogous to the unseen phase of incubation.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: The overlaid cover supplies the containing layer.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft fertile ground supplies a receiving and potentially generative substrate.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supplies the prepared bed beneath the cover.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A wide splitting with outward release supplies the later emergence through the containing layer.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The cultivator who cuts the earth supplies the agricultural transformation of concealment into yield.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Increase and growth supply the latent process occurring beneath the cover.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion into a hidden place supplies burial, whose value remains ambivalent until its outcome appears.

## punitive overrun
- reading: The cover can carry the latent force of an overrun: it comes over the field like a blow and leaves a leveled consequence.
- mechanism: Overflow beyond bounds, an incapacitating strike, eradication, leveling, and punishment strengthen the focus branch of overwhelming affliction. Night's cover can be carried as a force that overruns the field, disables resistance, and leaves a leveled aftermath.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B002: An affliction that envelops and overtakes supplies the total scope of the destructive reading.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B006: Making a whip or sword strike come over someone supplies the forceful impact hidden inside covering.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Water rising beyond its limit supplies the mechanics of an overrun that exceeds containment.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs supplies incapacitation at the point of support.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the endpoint of total overtaking.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalizing supplies the leveled result left across the whole affected field.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B007: Punishment following an offense supplies consequence as the trailing edge of the overrun.

## allotted temporal turn
- reading: Night covers by taking its allotted shift over -hā within a regulated succession of phases.
- mechanism: Ordered following, an assigned share of access to water, and succession activate a distributive reading. Night covers -hā by assuming its allotted temporal turn over the shared field, then yielding to the next claimant.
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B003: A night deictically tied to the current day supplies a bounded temporal slot.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B003: Coming upon a target supplies the act by which the phase assumes its turn.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Ordered following supplies a sequence of successive temporal claimants.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of watering and its channel supplies the concrete model of allotted access.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Replacement and succession supply the yielding of one turn to the next.

## conjugal generation
- reading: At the lexical edge, nocturnal covering can resonate as conjunction that initiates construction, birth, growth, and nurture.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B002: Action undertaken by night supplies the temporal setting for the generative encounter.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B004: Conjugal intercourse expressed as coming over a woman supplies the surprising focus-anchored relation.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B006: Building upon one's wife and entering marriage supplies a transition from conjunction to formed household.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B005: The coming forth of a child supplies the embodied consequence of generation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply development after generation.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Nurturing, repairing, and bringing to completion supply the sustaining end of the chain.

## luminous white cover
- reading: A contained branch reading lets night carry a white luminous mask across a dark ground, so covering can mark by contrast as well as obscure.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night supplies the expected dark ground against which the white-cover reversal becomes visible.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B007: Whiteness covering an animal's whole face supplies a luminous mask while preserving the focus verb's overlay.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B002: Moon-white coloration supplies a plausible nocturnal substance for the bright overlay.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B007: The whiteness and clarity of day supply the opposite luminous pole echoed inside the nocturnal mask.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The explicit she-camel image supplies a remote animal-form resonance without fixing the earlier pronoun's antecedent.

## covered assault on her
- reading: As contained foreshadowing, the nocturnal coming-over can cast the shadow of an arriving blow against a later protected feminine body.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B002: Nocturnal action supplies the possible cover or occasion under which an approach is hidden.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B003: Coming upon a target supplies the approach to the feminine object.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B006: Making a weapon or whip come over someone supplies the latent violent completion of that approach.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The she-camel's concrete form supplies the later feminine body echoed by -hā.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: Her allotted watering share supplies the protected claim violated by the later action.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting the animal's legs supplies the explicit incapacitating act that retroactively darkens the focus verb.

## internal grip
- reading: At the somatic edge, night-cover can model an inward grip that engulfs life and awareness from within.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B003: An inner gripping condition supplies engulfment from within rather than a veil applied from outside.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B004: Flowing blood as the support of life supplies the bodily interior in which the grip is felt.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Reason and inward awareness supply the faculty whose access can be engulfed.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Swallowing and taking something in fully supply the inward direction and totality of the seizure.


# Focus 91:5

## assembled canopy
- reading: The verse swears by a joined and supported upper canopy, making the sky's sustained structure—not merely its existence—the object of attention.
- mechanism: The sky is an upper cover whose stability comes from joined components and settled supports. The oath therefore foregrounds an engineered, load-bearing enclosure rather than undifferentiated empty height.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The branch supplies the sky as an overhead cover and gives the mechanism its enclosing upper surface.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: The branch supplies construction by joining parts and makes assembly the canopy's operative principle.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B009: The ribs and house-posts branch supplies load-bearing supports and turns generic building into a stable frame.

## visible constitution
- reading: Building denotes the sky's continuing constitution as a visible body whose outline displays how it has been formed.
- mechanism: A raised outline becomes the visible body of an underlying constitution. Building is thus not only an event in the past but a made form that remains perceptible in the sky's profile.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The looming raised outline supplies the sky's perceptible profile and makes elevation visually legible.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B013: The non-dominant mapped branch supplies the visible body or upper form, sharpening the sky from location into embodied contour.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B002: The built-form and constitution branch supplies the hidden organization expressed by that visible contour.

## readable mark
- reading: The sky is also a constructed trace: an organized mark that invites inference from visible form to the agency or order behind it.
- mechanism: The built sky can function as an identifying mark: its organized form is not merely seen but read as evidence of a condition or source. This is a split-root activation, not a claim that the surface noun lexically means a mark.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: The visible identifying mark supplies the artifact-like trace by which the sky becomes recognizable.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The sign read by discerning observation supplies the act of inference from the sky's visible organization.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B002: The branch supplies a constituted form whose organization can carry the identifying sign.

## weather builds life
- reading: The sky is a productive construction whose rain continues building life below and leaves growth as its visible mark.
- mechanism: The sky's building is dynamic and outwardly productive: the upper cover releases rain that marks land with growth, and nourishment in turn builds living flesh. Construction becomes a vertical life-making process rather than a finished roof.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The sky-cover branch explicitly extends to cloud, rain, and rain-dependent vegetation, supplying the downward life-bearing flow.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: The first rain marking earth with vegetation supplies visible growth as the sky's terrestrial imprint.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B010: Food building flesh supplies the conversion of nourishment into living structure.

## light cycle operates canopy
- reading: The sky is an operating envelope whose build becomes perceptible as it sequences light, unveiling, and concealment.
- mechanism: The assembled canopy becomes an operable light-envelope. Sun and moon occupy a sequence in which following, unveiling, and covering are recurrent modes; the sky's construction is experienced through controlled transitions rather than through static mass.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The overhead cover supplies the envelope within which alternating visibility can operate.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joined construction supplies an organized system rather than a sequence of unrelated appearances.
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: The sun and its brightness supply the primary luminous state of the system.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its celestial light supply a second luminous body within the same envelope.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession supply temporal linkage between luminous states.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance supply the system's revealing operation.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A cover rising over and concealing a thing supplies the complementary obscuring operation.

## horizon profile
- reading: The sky can also be seen as a built horizon-profile that makes celestial following spatially legible.
- mechanism: A split-root hill image spatializes the moon's following: the sky's raised outline becomes a profiled horizon along which a luminous follower appears to travel. The activation converts temporal succession into visible topography.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The raised outline visible from afar supplies the sky's horizon-like profile.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its light supply the moving visible figure traced against that profile.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: The non-dominant mapped hill branch supplies an elevated contour on which following can be spatially pictured.

## upper lower habitat
- reading: The sky is the upper half of a coordinated habitat, built in relation to an earth deliberately spread beneath it.
- mechanism: Building above and spreading below are complementary operations. The sky is no longer an isolated edifice: its upper cover and the extended lower surface jointly produce the bounded interval in which life can stand, move, and be seen.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The overhead cover supplies the upper boundary of the paired habitat.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B009: Settled supports supply stability to the upper boundary rather than mere height.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower surface explicitly opposed to the sky supplies the complementary boundary.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extension supply the horizontal operation paired with vertical construction.

## cosmos self constitution
- reading: The sky's constitution is an organized whole analogous to a completed self: outer form expresses an ordered interior.
- mechanism: The sky's built constitution becomes the cosmic member of a scale analogy. A self has an identity and inward faculty that is straightened and completed; the sky likewise has a made form whose organized wholeness, not just material bulk, is being invoked.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B002: Built form and constitution supply the focus-side concept carried into the analogy.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B012: The thing itself or its identity supplies a constituted whole at the human scale.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The inward faculty of reason and awareness supplies an interior organization, not merely an outer body.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Straightness and completion in the thing itself supply the finishing operation that clarifies constitution.

## thresholded moral capacity
- reading: Building also equips a formed whole with regulated openings and protections, making capacity and boundary part of the sky's constitution.
- mechanism: Constitution includes controlled thresholds. Inward intake is followed by two opposed tendencies: a breach that splits open and a protection that wards harm. Applied back to بَنَىٰ, building means designing passages, boundaries, and counter-capacities, not merely accumulating material.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B002: Built constitution anchors the threshold model in the focus verb.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Swallowing and taking something fully inward supply an image of content entering a constituted interior.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A wide split and eruption supply the breaching or opening capacity.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding harm with protection supplies the opposed containing capacity.

## cultivation or burial
- reading: The sky initiates a cultivable life-process whose created potential can be opened into growth or actively buried.
- mechanism: The weather-building baseline becomes a cultivation cycle. Rain marks land, the cultivator opens it, and growth increases; the opposed act pushes the life-bearing potential down into concealment. Building is therefore an ongoing choice between bringing form to growth and burying it.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: Early rain marking the earth with vegetation supplies the visible beginning of cultivation.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B010: Nourishment building flesh supplies growth as a genuine mode of construction.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The cultivator splitting earth supplies the opening action that lets marked growth develop.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Increase and growth supply the successful continuation of life-building.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion and concealment supply the reversal in which potential growth is pushed out of sight.

## bounded water order
- reading: The built sky also embodies measure: it contains and apportions life-bearing flow against the destructive possibility of overflow.
- mechanism: The rain-bearing sky is re-read as hydraulic order: construction holds and distributes water within measure. Overstepping appears materially as water rising into a sweeping excess, while a watering share and its channel represent apportioned flow.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The overhead sky branch extends to cloud and rain, supplying the water-bearing reservoir.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joined construction supplies containment and coordinated parts for ordered distribution.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force materialize exceeded measure as overflow.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of irrigation and its channel supply measured allocation rather than unrestricted flow.

## built form as testimony
- reading: The sky's construction acts as evidence-bearing testimony whose indication can be discerned, articulated, or denied.
- mechanism: The marked sky becomes nonverbal testimony. A thing can 'say' through what it indicates, a messenger makes indication explicit, and denial shows that visible evidence still requires a reader. The built form functions like a message without collapsing into spoken words.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: A sign read by discerning observation supplies the sky's capacity to be interpreted.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B002: Constituted form supplies the organized medium that carries the indication.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing a thing or speaker to falsehood supplies the possibility of refusing or discrediting evidence.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's 'saying' as its indication supplies the bridge from visible form to nonverbal proposition.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply the explicit communicative counterpart to the sky's implicit indication.

## builder as governing agent
- reading: One reading now hears a governing and completing agent behind the build, while the oath-by-building reading remains fully live beside it.
- mechanism: Later divine naming and lordship pull one live hearing of وَمَا بَنَىٰهَا toward an agent who owns, nurtures, repairs, and completes. This sharpens agency without cancelling the coexisting masdariyya hearing, 'and its building.'
- trace:
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: The active construction branch supplies the focus action whose subject remains grammatically open in مَا.
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped deity branch supplies explicit divine naming later in the same sequence.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Lordship, ownership, and sovereignty supply a personal governing agent.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion recast building as continuing governance rather than a one-time fabrication.

## covenantal supports
- reading: The built sky also models relational stability: an order stands through honored supports and can collapse when its foundational allocations are cut.
- mechanism: The physical support model acquires a social analogue. Message and a defined water share are load-bearing relations; hamstringing and cutting at the root attack those supports; eradication is the resulting collapse, while lordship names the authority that had nurtured and completed the order.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B009: Ribs and house-posts supply the load-bearing schema transferred to communal relations.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply an articulated rule that can support coordinated action.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The watering share and channel supply a concrete allocation around which the social structure is organized.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs supplies the literal destruction of supports.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B013: The root or foundation of a thing broadens the attack from a limb to structural origin.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies total collapse after the load-bearing relation is cut.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion supply the constructive governance against which collapse is measured.

## ordering can reverse to leveling
- reading: Building belongs to a wider power of measured ordering whose other face can level a formed order after its supports fail.
- mechanism: Making whole and making level coexist within the same ordering field. The self is completed; later, after eradication, the same root is applied again. This makes بَنَىٰ morally and materially non-sentimental: measured ordering can constitute differentiated form, while its punitive inverse can erase differentiation.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joining parts supplies constructive differentiation as the starting operation.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion supply ordering as formation.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the destructive condition under which ordering changes valence.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and equivalence supply the flattening possibility of making all distinctions level.

## artifact as aftermath trace
- reading: The sky is also the lasting footprint of building, a visible consequence that continues to identify the scale and asymmetry of its source.
- mechanism: The marked artifact can be viewed as an enduring aftermath of agency. The built sky is a visible remainder or footprint of an act, while the later sovereign agent is described in relation to fear and consequence. The reading shifts attention from the instant of building to what the act leaves standing behind it.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: The visible identifying mark supplies the sky as a durable signature of an act.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Construction supplies the originating act whose remainder is under consideration.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Sovereign lordship supplies the agentive asymmetry behind both ordering and consequence.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fear as anticipation of harm supplies the possibility of an agent recoiling from an act's result.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Remainder and trace supply the material persistence of what follows an act.

## tensioned animal canopy
- reading: The built sky can be imagined as a curved body under tension, held by rib-like supports whose integrity is essential to the whole.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The branch's extension from overhead sky to an animal's upper back permits an anatomical canopy image.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B005: The bow pressing against its string supplies stored tension in a curved built form.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B009: Ribs and posts supply the internal members that keep the tensioned form standing.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The camel's embodied form supplies the context-side organism on supports.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Hamstringing supplies the catastrophic result of cutting a load-bearing member.

## bridal generating chamber
- reading: The sky can be carried as a generative enclosure: a covered chamber within whose patterned alternation life and pairing become possible.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The overhead cover supplies the enclosing cosmic chamber.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B004: The hide dome or curtain-like shelter supplies the chamber's material form.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B006: Consummation under the bridal shelter supplies generation as an event enabled by enclosure.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B004: The intercourse branch of covering supplies a context-side convergence of enclosure and generation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B005: Pair and coupling supply the relational condition under which the generative chamber image coheres.

## celestial gathering calendar
- reading: The built sky can also be temporal architecture, a visible calendar that marks seasons around which collective life can gather.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: The marked gathering season supplies calendrical and communal organization.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Construction by joining parts supplies an ordered celestial framework rather than an isolated time-marker.
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: The sun and its brightness supply one observable calendrical regulator.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its light supply a second observable regulator.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession turn the lights into a repeatable sequence.

## nocturnal field of pursuit
- reading: At the far exploratory edge, the built sky becomes a nocturnal field that enables pursuit, following, and upward contest.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B006: Going out to hunt supplies pursuit under the sky as a latent activity.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B007: Rivaling by vying upward supplies contest within the elevated field.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joined construction keeps the pursuit image anchored in an ordered arena rather than a free-floating hunting theme.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B003: Hunting or setting out in moonlight supplies the context condition that activates the focus hunting branch.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following supplies pursuit's directional sequence.


# Focus 91:6

## lower expanse
- reading: The oath isolates the making of an extended lower plane, defined relationally against what is above.
- mechanism: The two roots compose a spatial operation: the lower member of a sky-ground relation is extended outward into an expanse.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower ground opposite the sky supplies the spatial patient and its below-ward orientation.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extension supply the outward operation performed on that lower ground.

## fertile receptive mat
- reading: The act lays out a receptive, fertile mat whose extension enables rooting, dwelling, and use.
- mechanism: Extension is functional rather than merely geometric: it lays out a soft, receptive substrate on which life can root, feed, and travel.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft fertile ground supplies a living, growth-bearing quality to the object being spread.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B005: The thick mat supplies a laid-out, habitable material analogy for the ground.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and widening turn the fertile material into an available surface.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B006: Sprawling along and adhering to the ground supplies the close surface-contact by which vegetation or bodies occupy it.

## grounded motion field
- reading: The spread earth is also a field that organizes rest, departure, distance, and return-like circulation.
- mechanism: The earth functions as a reference field: bodies may rest against it, be carried far across it, or circle over it, so spreading organizes trajectories as well as area.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B006: Sticking to the ground and lingering supply the fixed pole from which motion departs.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B002: Extended going supplies long-range movement across the field.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B003: Circling vultures supply a recurrent, non-linear trajectory around a grounded center.

## social pressure arena
- reading: Its extension can also be heard as making room for collective presence, pressure, and confrontation.
- mechanism: Making an expanse also makes an arena: collective bodies can assemble there, press against one another, and contest exposure or position.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B007: Coming forward in confrontation converts ground from scenery into a site of encounter.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B004: People pushing one another supplies the social force operating across the site.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B005: A great multitude or broad army supplies collective scale to the spreading.

## reversible flattening
- reading: The creative expanse carries a latent reversible image of being leveled down to the ground.
- mechanism: Spreading has a dangerous edge: the same low, extended profile can result from generative laying-out or from a body being pressed down and destroyed.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower ground supplies the terminal plane against which a body may be flattened.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B006: A struck body or animal sprawling against the ground supplies the violent route to flat extension.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B007: The isolated perishing sense keeps destruction live at the far edge of the verb.

## temporal visibility field
- reading: It is a temporally modulated visibility field, repeatedly exposed, traversed, disclosed, and veiled.
- mechanism: Before the earth appears, exposure, following, uncovering, and covering establish a recurring visibility cycle. The spread surface becomes the field on which those temporal changes can register.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower ground supplies the visible field affected by changing light.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B003: Circular motion supplies a recurrent rather than merely linear mode of traversal.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight supplies the phase in which the field becomes exposed.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession supply temporal ordering between phases.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and manifestation make visibility an active operation upon the field.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering laid over something supplies the inverse phase that veils the field.

## built cosmic floor
- reading: Its spreading is the lower, load-bearing counterpart to the sky's construction within a coordinated cosmic architecture.
- mechanism: The adjacent sky-building line turns the earth's extension into the complementary lower operation of one constructed whole: assembly and support above, laid-out availability below.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: Ground opposite the sky supplies the lower architectural member.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading supplies the floor-like operation complementary to building.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: What rises and overshadows supplies the upper member of the paired structure.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joining parts into a building supplies deliberate assembly rather than accidental extension.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B009: Ribs or supports on which something rests supply a load-bearing analogy for the ordered whole.

## proportioned earth body
- reading: It names a mode of earth-forming in which extension, smoothness, balance, and fitness belong to one proportioning act.
- mechanism: The next syntactic twin shifts from earth to self and from spreading to making even and complete. Its branch inventory unexpectedly returns to wide smooth land, causing physical extension and inward proportioning to cross-interpret one another.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and widening supply the earth-side shaping operation.
