  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B012: The very self or identity of a thing supplies the inward whole being shaped.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Straightness and intrinsic completion supply proportion and integrity, not mere flatness.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B006: A just middle supplies balance and measured placement within the formed whole.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Wide smooth ground directly loops the soul-forming root back into the earth's spatial profile.

## cultivable surface
- reading: Spreading prepares a cultivable substrate whose completion remains potential until it is opened, worked, and made to grow.
- mechanism: Wide splitting, the ploughman's cleavage of earth, and growth form a material sequence: opening the laid-out substrate makes its latent fertility productive.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft fertile ground supplies the latent capacity for cultivation.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading makes the fertile substrate broadly available for subsequent operations.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Wide fissuring and eruption supply the first opening of the substrate.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The ploughman who cleaves earth converts opening into deliberate cultivation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the productive result of working the opened ground.

## growth or burial
- reading: The same receptive expanse is morally ambivalent: it can bring capacity into growth or become the cover under which capacity is suppressed.
- mechanism: Growth and covert insertion establish opposite relations to the same receptive surface: one brings capacity upward into increase, while the other presses it out of sight.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Fertile ground supplies a substrate capable of either supporting or covering life.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B006: Close adherence and flattening against the surface supply the pressure by which something can be kept down.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the upward, manifest outcome.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion and concealment supply the inverse operation of burying capacity beneath view.

## bounded expansion
- reading: The earth's spreading is measured extensibility; force that outruns measure becomes the destructive counter-image of overflow.
- mechanism: Two mapped inventories converge on water crossing its limit. Against that surge, the focus act reads less as limitless expansion than as extension held within a proportioned field.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and widening supply extension whose proper measure is being tested.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B006: A just middle supplies the contextual idea of measured placement.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force supply the dominant image of extension escaping its boundary.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: The second mapped root independently preserves overflowing water, strengthening the excess-versus-measure contrast.

## mobilized social ground
- reading: Its spread is an enabling social field on which stillness can become directed movement, crowd pressure, and irreversible action.
- mechanism: The stirring of what was still and the dispatch of a group activate the focus branches of far-going, crowd scale, and mutual pushing. The expanse becomes a launchable field for consequential action.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B007: Ground as a place of confrontation supplies the social site into which action emerges.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B004: Mutual pushing supplies the force generated when agents occupy the field together.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B005: A multitude or broad army supplies the collective scale of the field.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Stirring the stationary from rest supplies the transition from latent presence to action.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: A group's forceful setting-out supplies directed collective movement across the arena.

## apportioned common ground
- reading: It is a resource-bearing common whose extension creates shares, channels, routes, and duties of restraint.
- mechanism: Gentle conveyance, animal management, and a defined share or channel of water transform fertile expanse into a governed common. Physical room entails access, pacing, and restraint.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Fertile ground supplies the shared resource-bearing territory.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading supplies the extent across which access must be organized.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B004: Gentleness and deliberation supply the regulated manner appropriate to sharing the field.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B004: Taming and management supply the human-animal relation operating within the common.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A water share and its channel supply concrete allocation across the land.

## annihilative leveling reversal
- reading: Constructive laying-out and catastrophic leveling become reversible profiles: what is made fit and extended can also be brought uniformly down.
- mechanism: The narrative performs the dark edge already latent in the focus branches: lower limbs are cut, a body is brought to ground, annihilation spreads over the group, and equalization closes the sequence.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: Ground together with an animal's lower legs and hoof-side supplies the bodily interface later attacked.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B006: A struck body sprawling against the earth supplies the posture produced by the attack.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B007: Perishing supplies the terminal edge that the later annihilation makes active.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs supplies the concrete loss of upright support.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Total eradication scales the fallen-body image from one victim to the community.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization supplies the final leveling operation and reverses constructive proportion into undifferentiated outcome.

## aftermath bearing surface
- reading: Even leveled ground can carry the reach, track, and remainder of what occurred upon it.
- mechanism: Heel, trace, sequel, and remainder resist reading the final leveling as pure erasure. The extended ground becomes a medium on which an act continues as aftermath.
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The ground supplies the surface on which tracks and remnants can persist.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Extension supplies the spatial and temporal reach over which aftermath can continue.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B002: Heel and footprint supply the material image of an act leaving a track.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The end or consequence supplies temporal continuation beyond the act.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Remainder and trace keep a residue live after apparent leveling.

## rain inscribed ground
- reading: It is a receptive sheet that sky-borne rain can inscribe into visible vegetation.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Fertile ground supplies the receptive material that can answer rain with vegetation.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading supplies the broad sheet over which the mark can appear.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: First rain marking earth with plants supplies the sky-to-ground inscription event.

## circumferential spread
- reading: A faint coexisting image treats expanse as organized by repeated following and circulation around a grounded reference.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: Ground supplies the center or lower reference around which recurrent motion is perceived.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B003: Vultures circling supply the focus verb's own circumferential trajectory.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its sky-light supply the moving celestial participant.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession supply recurrence and ordered pursuit.

## hydrological catchment
- reading: It can be imagined as a catchment laid out from river incision through allotted channels to the tails of valleys.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Fertile ground supplies the terrain whose productivity depends on distributed water.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extension supply the area across which the network runs.
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B001: A flowing river cleaving earth supplies the upstream incision of the terrain.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of irrigation and its channel supply controlled distribution.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B013: Abundant water supplies the volume that the terrain must receive and route.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B004: Watercourses and the tails of valleys supply downstream endings and distributary reach.

## concealed decay under extent
- reading: A wide surface can conceal tiny local corruption that hollows the whole until collapse becomes sudden and total.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B010: A termite silently eating wood supplies hidden structural hollowing beneath an intact appearance.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B011: A wound festering with pus supplies corruption that grows beneath a surface.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B005: Great breadth and mass supply the large visible whole vulnerable to local hidden decay.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Covert insertion supplies concealment as the mechanism by which damage escapes notice.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the eventual whole-system collapse at the end of hidden deterioration.


# Focus 91:7

## living whole
- reading: A living whole, together with the act or agent that brought its own constitution into internally sound completion.
- mechanism: The living soul-self is not presented as a bare unit but together with the act or agent that gives it internal straightness and sound completion. The paired oath therefore foregrounds both an achieved living whole and the formative relation that makes it coherent.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B011: The living soul-self supplies the animate whole whose constitution is being considered.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal straightness and sound completion supply the formative operation that integrates the living whole.

## interior equilibrium
- reading: The self's hidden cognitive and intentional powers were calibrated into a workable internal relation.
- mechanism: The focus can describe calibration within an interior field: thought, intention, and discernment are placed into measured relation rather than left as an undifferentiated substance. This is structural equilibrium, not yet a verdict that every impulse is morally equal.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The inner mind, thought, and hidden intent make the oath's object an inward field of discernment.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and equivalence supply measured relation among distinguishable inward powers.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B006: The middle, fairness, and even meeting place make equilibrium a relational center rather than featureless sameness.

## developmental completion
- reading: Living emergence was brought toward mature strength, form, and intelligence through a completing process.
- mechanism: The shaping can be read temporally: emergent life is carried toward mature bodily, intellectual, and dispositional capacity. Completion is then a process of becoming able, not merely an instantaneous geometric arrangement.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B005: Birth and postpartum emergence provide the developmental starting edge of the self.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B011: The living soul-self carries continuity between emergence and later capacity.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B005: Reaching full bodily and intellectual maturity supplies the developmental endpoint of taswiya.

## oriented intent
- reading: The self was fitted with inward intention and the capacity to orient and aim, without predetermining its chosen target.
- mechanism: Taswiya may include orienting rather than only balancing: an inwardly intending self is furnished with the capacity to turn toward and aim at a direction. The focus then concerns a directional agent, while leaving the direction and its later use open.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Hidden intent supplies the interior source from which direction can arise.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B004: Turning toward a direction supplies an orienting function for the formative act.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B008: Aiming toward a person or direction sharpens orientation into targetable intention.

## breathing open system
- reading: The self is an open, breathing process made sound through regulated expansion, exchange, and relief.
- mechanism: The self can be carried as an open living process whose integrity depends on patterned exchange: breath exits, constriction is relieved by room, and openings spread without destroying the whole. Taswiya becomes dynamic regulation of expansion and return rather than immobile symmetry.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B001: Breath leaving the body supplies rhythmic exchange as a literal image of living selfhood.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B002: Relief by giving breath and space makes room an enabling condition for continued life.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B009: Opening and spreading like breath supply expansion that can remain part of a regulated whole.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Sound internal completion contains the respiratory imagery within an organized living system.

## cyclic equilibration
- reading: Taswiya gives the inward self coherence across recurring phases of emergence, disclosure, concealment, and return.
- mechanism: The ordered light sequence changes interior equilibrium from a static midpoint into serial regulation. Illumination emerges, a second light follows, day opens and reveals, then night covers; an inward self can accordingly remain coherent across alternating states of disclosure and concealment.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The hidden mind and intent provide the focus-ayah interior in which disclosure and concealment can alternate.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equivalence supplies the balancing relation that the context turns from static symmetry into a sequence.
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B001: The sun and its brightness supply the primary pole of overt illumination.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Emergence into sunlight and visibility make illumination an event of coming forth.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B001: The moon and its light supply a distinct succeeding mode of illumination.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession turn the two lights into an ordered transition rather than a loose pair.
  - 91:3 **وَٱلنَّهَارِ** ن ه ر B002: Day opening through light supplies an expansive phase in the regulatory cycle.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance supply the cycle's disclosure function.
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: Night and its darkness supply the contrasting phase rather than a defect in the cycle.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering that rises over and conceals supplies controlled occlusion as the reverse operation of disclosure.

## composite habitat
- reading: The self is a composed, load-bearing, internally spacious habitat whose differentiated regions were fitted into one whole.
- mechanism: The sky-building and earth-spreading pair activates the self as a composite habitat. Height, joined construction, supporting members, a lower field, and extension together make taswiya the production of an internally inhabitable space with differentiated regions and room for movement.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B012: The very thing itself anchors the architectural analogy in the self's whole identity rather than in an external building.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B015: Leeway, distance, and spaciousness give the self an extensible interior rather than point-like identity.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Wide smooth ground supplies a focus-internal spatial image onto which the earth sequence can latch.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: What is high and overshadows supplies an upper enclosure for the habitat analogy.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Building by joining parts supplies composition as the causal mechanism of internal order.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B002: A composite structure supplies differentiated organization rather than homogeneous mass.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B009: Ribs or supports on which something rests supply load-bearing relations among the self's parts.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B001: The lower counterpart to the sky supplies the habitat's grounding field.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supply the production of room in which an organized self can act.

## moral threshold regulator
- reading: The self was made as an intact yet permeable threshold-system able to recognize both boundary-breaking release and self-protective restraint.
- mechanism: The immediate sequel turns inward proportion into a threshold system. The self receives or internalizes opposed possibilities; one is imaged as fissure, sudden opening, and breach of a cover, while the other is damage-preventing protection. Taswiya therefore equips a bounded but permeable self to register both breach and safeguarding, not to endorse them equally.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The inner mind and hidden intent provide the receiving and discerning interior.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B009: Opening and spreading supply an aperture whose regulation can succeed or fail.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal straightness and completion supply the intact condition against which breach and protection become legible.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Swallowing and taking something in fully supply a material image for inward reception or assimilation.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A broad fissure and bursting forth supply the literal mechanics of a threshold opening.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Deviation from right and rupture of a covering turn the opening into a morally charged breach.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding off harm by protection supplies the opposed boundary-preserving operation.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Placing oneself within protection makes safeguarding a self-involving response rather than an external wall alone.

## cultivable self
- reading: Taswiya prepares a living and workable field whose later growth and purification still depend on cultivation.
- mechanism: The earlier fertile earth and spreading images combine with the later ploughman, success, growth, and purification branches. The self's taswiya becomes preparation of an arable field: initial formation supplies workable ground, while later cultivation can split, aerate, clean, and increase what grows there.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B011: The living soul-self keeps the terrain analogy anchored in a living subject capable of growth.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Wide smooth open ground supplies the focus-side image of a field prepared for use.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Sound completion makes preparation a viable starting condition, not the crop's final moral state.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft fertile earth supplies the material substrate capable of receiving cultivation.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supply the preparation of an available field.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The ploughman who cuts the earth supplies active cultivation rather than passive possession of good soil.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success and enduring survival supply the functional yield of cultivation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the crop-like result of working the self.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B002: Purity and soundness add clearing and repair to the growth mechanism.

## depth visibility
- reading: The self has depth and a presentational surface: its formed interior can be disclosed and polished or concealed and corrupted.
- mechanism: Revelation and polishing oppose covering and hidden insertion, while purification names repair. These operations activate a layered self with surface and depth: taswiya establishes an inner structure that can be made manifest and polished, or pushed under and corrupted without ceasing to be the self's own interior.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Hidden thought and intent supply the depth that can later be disclosed or buried.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal soundness supplies the latent order affected by polishing, covering, purification, or corruption.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance supply movement from depth into visibility.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B002: Polishing and clarification supply an operation that improves how inner order appears.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering that conceals supplies reversible occlusion of what remains underneath.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B002: Purity and repair supply restoration of the self's latent sound order.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Insertion into hiding supplies the downward action by which the self is buried beneath its presentation.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: Misguidance and corruption make concealment deformative rather than merely protective.

## hydraulic latent activation
- reading: The self is a reservoir of latent energies whose taswiya regulates activation and flow, while transgression is an overpowering overflow beyond that order.
- mechanism: The threshold reading acquires pressure and kinetics. Sudden abundant release, protection, water exceeding its bounds, and a dormant thing being stirred into motion model the self as a regulated reservoir of latent capacities. Transgression becomes an activated overflow across a limit, not a foreign substance inserted from outside.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B009: Opening, swelling water, and waves throwing out water supply a focus-internal pressure and release image.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal straightness supplies the regulated condition from which overflow departs.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B003: A large quantity rushing out suddenly supplies pressure release as a latent-to-actual transition.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Damage-preventing protection supplies containment and controlled response.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Exceeding a limit in disobedience names the failure of regulation in moral terms.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Water rising into overpowering overflow supplies the hydraulic mechanics of limit-exceeding.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Stirring what is still from dormancy supplies the activation of a previously latent capacity.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: A group's forceful setting-out supplies the kinetic surge that follows activation.

## signifying dispatch
- reading: The self was fitted to formulate, signify, and dispatch intention into the world, with a built-in possibility of truthful transmission or representational spoofing.
- mechanism: Denial, inner speech, signification, and sending activate the self as a representational routing system. Hidden intent can be formulated inwardly, encoded into outward signs or speech, and dispatched into action; the same interface can be spoofed by a lying self or deceptive covering.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Inner thought and hidden intent supply the message source within the focus self.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B004: Turning toward a direction supplies routing from inward intent toward an outward target.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B008: The lying self supplies the possibility that the internal source can falsify its own representation.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B009: A garment deceptive in its appearance supplies a surface signal that can misrepresent what lies beneath.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B012: Speech held within the self supplies pre-articulate internal formulation.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's saying as its signification supplies the conversion of inner state into a legible sign.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B001: Sending and setting forth supply transmission from the self toward another.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply an explicit carrier-content relation for the routing model.

## agentive nurture
- reading: The clause more strongly evokes purposive lordly formation and nurture, while still allowing the proportioning act itself to remain the oath's object.
- mechanism: The later naming of deity and lord, especially lordship joined to repair, nurture, and completion, sharpens the agentive side of وَمَا سَوَّىٰهَا. The focus may swear by a purposive completing agency and not only by an impersonal pattern, while the source construction still permits the act of proportioning itself to remain foregrounded.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B011: The living soul-self supplies the recipient of sustained formative agency.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Sound completion supplies the kind of formative work that can be attributed to a purposive agent.
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B001: Deity and worship supply an explicitly divine agentive horizon later in the sequence.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Lordship, ownership, and sovereignty supply agency capable of imposing order.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and bringing to completion closely match the focus act's completing branch.

## sustained carrier
- reading: Taswiya can also image adaptive fitting: a water-dependent living carrier is given supports, capacities, and measured provision suited to movement and burden.
- mechanism: The she-camel and its watering activate two unusually concrete focus branches: a camel-back support under س و ي and breath-sized or life-sustaining drink under ن ف س. Taswiya can therefore be imaged as adaptive fitting of an embodied carrier whose movement and burden-bearing depend on support, management, and an apportioned channel of water.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B006: A drinking breath or draught supplies paced intake by which an embodied self is sustained.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B008: Life-sustaining water or drink makes hydration constitutive of continued living selfhood.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B010: The camel-back pad or pack cloth supplies fitted support that distributes a carrier's load.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The she-camel and its bodily form provide the concrete living carrier that activates the focus support image.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B004: Taming, training, and management supply functional fitting of living power to directed use.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B001: Quenching a drinker supplies the sustaining input required by the carrier.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An allotted share of watering and its channel supply measured provision rather than undirected abundance.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The non-dominant mapped image of leg and the carrier it supports intensifies the load-bearing analogy while remaining form-distant.

## destructive leveling
- reading: The self's taswiya is constructive determination of order by a power whose equalizing operation can elsewhere become terminal leveling; completion and erasure remain disturbing co-potentials of the root.
- mechanism: The exact سَوَّىٰ recurrence after hamstringing and annihilation reverses the emotional valence of the focus verb without erasing its constructive branch. Equalization and smooth ground can name either integration into sound form or reduction of differentiated life to a terminal level state. Taswiya is thus power to impose determinate order, not an intrinsically gentle synonym for improvement.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B012: The self as the very whole thing supplies the differentiated identity affected by constructive completion.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal straightness and sound completion preserve the constructive reading at the focus.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and equivalence supply the shared operation capable of taking either constructive or leveling valence.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting a beast's legs supplies disabling loss of differentiated movement before the later leveling.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B013: The root or base of a thing makes the destructive action reach structural foundations.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Annihilating eradication supplies the terminal context that flips equalization toward destructive leveling.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: The repeated equality branch carries the same root operation into the aftermath of eradication.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Wide smooth ground supplies the concrete image of differences flattened into one level field.

## temporal consequence self
- reading: The proportioned self is a time-spanning trajectory able to aim ahead, leave traces behind, and become answerable to what follows.
- mechanism: Following near the opening and aftermath at the close bracket the self with temporal extension. A deed has a tail, successors, traces, and an outcome that can be anticipated; taswiya can therefore include prospective orientation and retrospective continuity, making the self a trajectory rather than a momentary interior state.
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B015: Leeway, distance, length, and delay supply temporal extension within the focus root.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Discernment and hidden intent supply the capacity to project action toward a later result.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B004: Turning toward a direction makes formed selfhood prospective rather than temporally inert.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession establish the earliest context image of ordered temporal consequence.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: A thing's tail or ending supplies the trailing extension of an act conventionally named as guilt.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fear as alarm at expected harm supplies prospective modeling of a not-yet-present result.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B002: The rear of the foot and the trace it leaves supply embodied continuity behind movement.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Succession and one thing coming after another supply causal follow-through.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The end and outcome of a thing supply the terminal state toward which intention can be oriented.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remnant and lingering trace supply persistence of action after the acting moment.

## selective permeability
- reading: The self may be carried as a selectively permeable interior whose soundness depends on regulating what passes through its openings.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B001: Breath leaving the body requires a traversable boundary and supplies literal exchange.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: The hidden inward mind supplies an interior that is not identical with its boundary.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal soundness supplies regulated integrity despite exchange with what is outside.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: A narrow aperture through which something enters supplies the selective interface.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: A special property lodged in the interior supplies differentiation between inward constitution and external passage.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B005: Repair entering between disparate things supplies regulation at a boundary where differences meet.

## measured moral stake
- reading: The self can be imagined as a measured endowment entering real risk, where formed capacities do not abolish divergent success and failure.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B016: The named fifth gambling arrow supplies the odd but focus-internal image of an allotted stake.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and matching supply measurement among stakes or outcomes.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B007: Wagering and overcoming through deception activate a field of risk and contest around the rare focus branch.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success and enduring survival supply one consequential outcome.
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B001: Failure to attain the sought result supplies the opposing outcome.
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B002: A qidḥ that fails to kindle supplies a concrete image of latent potential that does not produce its hoped-for result.

## legible but spoofable surface
- reading: The formed self may carry outward signs of inward condition, yet its signifying surface can be obscured or deliberately spoofed.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: Hidden thought and intent supply an interior whose outward signs may reveal or misrepresent it.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Internal soundness supplies the condition against which faithful or deceptive appearance can be judged.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: A visible mark by which something becomes known supplies an outward legibility interface.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: A sign from which an observer infers condition supplies interpretation of surface evidence.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B008: The lying self supplies inward distortion of the signal.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B009: A garment that lies through its appearance supplies outward spoofing of the sign.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's saying as its signification supplies the interpretive bridge from visible mark to claimed inner state.

## generative continuity
- reading: Taswiya may also fit a life to become one generative link in succession, making the later cutting of root, mobility, and offspring a destruction of continuity itself.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B005: Birth and the newborn supply entry into generational continuity.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B005: Full maturity supplies the capacity-bearing stage between birth and possible continuation.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B004: Infertility and interruption of offspring supply direct severance of generative continuity.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B005: A final thing with nothing following it supplies terminality without successor.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B013: The root or base of a thing supplies the structural point at which continuity can be cut.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B004: Offspring or descendants supply the continuation that destructive barrenness removes.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Succession supplies the broader temporal chain in which a mature self can function as one link.


# Focus 91:8

## inward assimilation
- reading: The verse depicts a soul made to internalize two executable orientations—breaching restraint and taking protective position—as capacities belonging to its own constitution.
- mechanism: The soul is made to take in a paired interior repertoire: one mode breaches restraint and the other places the self within protection. Inspiration is therefore embodied assimilation of actionable orientations, not merely receipt of two definitions.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Complete swallowing supplies the intake image and makes the causative act an inward assimilation of the pair.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Departure from right through breached restraint supplies the outwardly transgressive orientation taken into the soul.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Placing oneself in protective caution supplies the counter-orientation as an enacted self-positioning.

## threshold pair
- reading: They are opposed operations on a shared threshold—rupture/outflow and protective interposition—installed within one capacious soul.
- mechanism: Fujur opens a wide rupture through which stored pressure can issue; taqwa interposes a guard against harm. Their coordination models moral agency as regulation of permeability: whether an interior force becomes uncontrolled outflow or remains behind a protective threshold.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Wide splitting and outflow provide the opening operation and its capacity to release what was contained.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: A barrier that wards off harm provides the opposed operation of preserving a boundary.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B004: Breadth and abundance make the inspired endowment capacious enough to contain both opposed boundary operations.

## asymmetric hazard
- reading: The construction may encode an asymmetric risk topology: an eruptive capacity that can engulf and a protective capacity meant to precede and contain it.
- mechanism: The pair is not dynamically symmetrical. Fujur can arrive as sudden multiplying pressure, while taqwa functions as prior protection against engulfment; the verse can thus map an internal hazard and its countermeasure rather than two equally placid options.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B005: An engulfing calamity supplies the hazard horizon within which inspiration includes recognition of what can consume the agent.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B003: A sudden bursting-in of many supplies the nonlinear escalation associated with the hazardous orientation.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding harm with a barrier supplies the anticipatory countermeasure rather than a second kind of eruption.

## cosmic visibility cycle
- reading: Its inspired discernment can be read as phased visibility: orientations emerge, become manifest, and can be protectively covered without ceasing to belong to the soul.
- mechanism: The ordered context supplies succession, uncovering, and covering. These operations revise the focus pair from fixed contents into alternating modes of moral visibility: rupture can break into disclosure, while protection can cover without erasing what is covered.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B002: Dawn breaking from night makes the focus noun capable of registering emergence into visibility as well as moral breach.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Protective covering supplies a bounded concealment that preserves rather than annihilates.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession make the reveal-cover relation temporal rather than a frozen opposition.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance provide the explicit disclosure phase that sharpens the dawn activation in fujur.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A cover rising over and veiling a thing supplies the reversible occlusion that distinguishes protection from destruction.

## constructed hinge
- reading: Inspiration is the interior hinge of the soul's formation: rupture and protection are built-in affordances of a completed creature, whose use is exposed before the later equalizing endpoint.
- mechanism: Assembly, spatial extension, and completed proportioning culminate immediately in inspiration; the later recurrence of equalizing after destruction gives the same root a terminal edge. The focus becomes the hinge inside a constructed order: an interior boundary system is installed after formation and tested before a final leveling.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Complete inward uptake makes inspiration the interior stage of the surrounding constructive sequence.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A wide opening supplies one affordance built into the completed interior.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: A protective barrier supplies the counter-affordance built into that same interior.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Building by joining parts supplies the assembly stage of the enclosing construction sequence.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supply an opened field within which the constructed soul can act.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Intrinsic straightness and completion place the inspired pair after formation rather than after a defect.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization at the narrative endpoint turns the earlier proportioning into one side of a formation-to-leveling frame.

## furrow not breach
- reading: Opening can become a fertile furrow or a destructive breach, and containment can become a protective barrier or corruptive burial; the inspired discernment concerns how an operation is governed, not just which geometry it has.
- mechanism: The same material operation—a split—can be a ruinous breach or a cultivated furrow. Growth follows opened and tended ground, whereas failure follows concealment and corruption. This revises moral polarity: openness is not intrinsically fujur, and containment is not intrinsically taqwa; purpose and care decide whether the boundary operation fructifies, protects, ruptures, or buries.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Wide splitting and outflow supply the raw opening whose moral direction is put under pressure by the later cultivation image.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: A harm-blocking barrier supplies protective containment and prevents it from being confused with mere burial.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Splitting and cutting repeat the focus opening at the point where the outcome is success.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The plowman splitting earth turns opening into a fertile furrow and supplies the constructive alternative to breach.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase provide the result of an opening that is cultivated rather than allowed to rupture.
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B001: Missing the sought result and being deprived of one's effort supply the failed outcome of the contrary treatment.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion and concealment provide burial-like containment, sharply distinct from a barrier that protects.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: Corruption and leading astray mark why this concealment is not taqwa despite their shared surface geometry of containment.

## latent to mobilized
- reading: They are action architectures: one can ignite into a fast, self-amplifying chain of embodied harm, while the other is a pre-action protective positioning.
- mechanism: The context turns an inward capacity into a motor sequence: stillness is stirred, movement launches, another body's movement is disabled, and the cascade ends in eradication. This strengthens the baseline claim that the inspired pair is executable; fujur is a transition from latent pressure to mobilized harm, while taqwa is the capacity to interrupt that transition.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B003: Sudden bursting-in supplies the nonlinear transition from an inner disposition to an event already in motion.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Deliberately placing the self in protection supplies the possible interruption before mobilization becomes injury.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing what was still provides the activation step that converts latent capacity into motion.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: An impulsive going-forth supplies the launched phase of the action cascade.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Disabling an animal's legs turns mobilized breach into the destruction of another's capacity for movement.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the terminal amplification of a breach that was not interrupted.

## inner outer testimony
- reading: It can be noncoercive inward testimony: public speech gives the assimilated distinction an external voice, while denial remains a possible active response.
- mechanism: Complete inward uptake is later mirrored by spoken indication and a messenger, while denial supplies an active counter-relation to truth. Inspiration can therefore function as inward testimony that does not coerce assent: an external saying can reactivate what was assimilated, and denial can oppose rather than erase it.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Complete intake makes the inspired distinction internally available before any later public utterance.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B001: Contradiction of truth supplies an active response to available indication rather than mere absence of information.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B012: A saying held within the self bridges inward apprehension and the explicit speech occurring in the narrative.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's own indication broadens speech into readable signification and lets the external warning echo an interior sign.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message provide the public counterpart to the focus verse's inwardly assimilated distinction.
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing a speaker or sign to falsehood supplies the deliberate reversal by which testimony is resisted.

## hydraulic limit
- reading: Fujur is unmanaged overflow that breaches a shared channel, whereas taqwa is ecological limit-keeping that protects apportioned access and downstream consequences.
- mechanism: The focus rupture/barrier pair is externalized as water governance. Force rises beyond a limit, watering has a share and channel, and downstream channels or a full bucket carry the flow onward. Fujur becomes pressure escaping its proper course; taqwa becomes preservation of banks, shares, and downstream life.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Wide splitting with water-like outflow supplies the breach through which pressure escapes.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: A harm-blocking barrier supplies the bank or guard that keeps force within a life-preserving limit.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and sweeping force make transgression a pressure that overtops its limit.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: The non-dominant mapped inventory independently preserves water-overflow as a convergent image for limit-crossing.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An allotted share of watering and its channel supply the bounded distribution that overflow violates.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B004: Water channels at valley-ends supply the downstream path along which a boundary failure propagates.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B007: A full bucket supplies a finite portion whose fullness can sustain allocation or become excess when mishandled.

## protected allotment
- reading: It also concerns relational boundaries: fujur violates another's protected body and allocation, while taqwa guards claims that do not originate in the self.
- mechanism: The protected animal and its watering allocation turn the focus opposition outward into a social and legal relation. The sanctity-violation branch of fujur becomes concrete as disabling what bears a protected claim; taqwa becomes maintaining a barrier around another being's body and share.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B006: Battle-days named for violated sanctity supply the public and legal scale of fujur as breach of an inviolable boundary.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: A guard against harm supplies taqwa as protection extended around a vulnerable being and its entitlement.
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B001: The worshipped and divine relation marks the animal and allocation as claims bearing more than private utility.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The camel's embodied form supplies the vulnerable claimant whose protected boundary can be honored or breached.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of watering and its course supply the specific distributive right guarded by the warning.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs supplies the bodily enactment of violating both protected life and protected access.

## consequence train
- reading: It distinguishes two trajectories whose acts carry a following tail, moral residue, and eventual outcome.
- mechanism: Following, tail/end, residue, and outcome activate a temporal reading of the possessive pair. Each orientation has a train that follows it: the inspired capacity is not exhausted in the instant of choice but includes awareness that acts acquire tails, traces, and aftermaths.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Outflow supplies an act whose effects can leave the initial breach and travel onward.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: A protective barrier supplies action taken in anticipation of what would otherwise follow.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B003: A remainder following what precedes supplies the first image of a trailing consequence.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B001: Culpability supplies the moral burden attached to the completed act.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The tail or final part of a thing turns culpability into an image of what trails behind action.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: A thing's end and outcome provide the explicit terminal reach of the inspired orientations.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Residue and trace supply the persistent wake left after the focal decision has passed.

## protection without panic
- reading: Taqwa is skilled protective orientation toward consequences; fear may accompany it, but the context prevents their simple identification.
- mechanism: The final negated fear separates taqwa's protective caution from panic. Taqwa is an active placement of the self behind a guard in view of consequences; fear is the affective expectation of harm. The former can organize conduct even where the latter is absent or belongs to a different subject.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Placing oneself in protective caution supplies deliberate conduct rather than involuntary alarm.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fright that anticipates something hated supplies the affective state from which protective taqwa is distinguished.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The final outcome supplies the future horizon toward which protective action can be oriented without collapsing into fear.

## guarded gait
- reading: Taqwa is careful moral foot-placement that preserves one's own and another's capacity to keep moving, while fujur culminates in disabling movement.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B003: Guarded gait around hoof pain makes protection a bodily skill of placing each step so movement remains possible.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The camel's concrete animal form gives the focus branch's otherwise remote gait image a visible body in the context.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: The cutting of an animal's legs supplies the exact reversal of guarded movement: another's ability to step is destroyed.

## calibrated share
- reading: It includes calibrating a just share and a fitting limit—protection by proportion rather than indiscriminate refusal.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B004: A known unit of weight supplies exact calibration as an exploratory material property of protective judgment.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and matching between two things provide the balancing operation needed for calibration.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An apportioned watering share supplies the concrete resource whose limit must be measured and protected.

## devouring ignition
- reading: It also names an inward activation with velocity: once the eruptive capacity is launched, it can consume moral distance and accelerate toward engulfing consequence.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B003: Speed that seems to devour ground supplies the startling possibility that inward uptake can become rapid propulsion.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B005: An engulfing calamity supplies the terminal hazard when that propulsion becomes indiscriminate consumption.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B003: Sudden bursting-in connects rapid internal propulsion specifically to the eruptive side of the inspired pair.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: Impulsive forward movement supplies the contextual ignition of the focus root's speed image.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the consuming endpoint that contains the model as a hazard trajectory rather than a neutral speed metaphor.

## interior mark
- reading: Inspiration installs an inward discriminant—a latent mark by which the soul can recognize the two capacities when they become active.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Complete assimilation supplies the interior location in which a discriminating mark or property could be installed.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mapped branch's visible mark supplies legibility: a distinction by which a thing becomes recognizable.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B003: The other non-dominant mapped branch's inward property relocates that legible distinction from an exterior sign to the interior.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Completion of the self supplies the formed substrate in which the interior distinction is placed.


# Focus 91:9

## lasting purification
- reading: Whoever actively brings the pronominal object into rectitude has already entered a success characterized by continuance in good.
- mechanism: Purity and rectitude are actively produced, while falāḥ names attainment that remains in good; the action is therefore both corrective and durative.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success as attainment, rescue, and lasting good makes the predicate a durable result rather than a passing win.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude supply the direct transforming operation performed upon the pronominal object.

## opened field growth
- reading: Purification is cultivation: opening a viable field and drawing its latent capacity into growth.
- mechanism: A compacted medium is opened like soil, then cultivated so that its own capacity can increase; success is the emergence made possible by that work.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving or opening supplies the first material operation: making passage through what was closed.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The tiller who splits ground turns opening into deliberate cultivation rather than mere rupture.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth, increase, and thriving provide the cultivated response that makes the opening successful.

## fitted capacity
- reading: The object is made fit to realize and sustain its proper capacity.
- mechanism: The agent succeeds by making the pronominal object apt for its proper work; rectitude is functional fit, not only spotless condition.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B004: Suitability and fittingness turn purification into calibration for a fitting condition or use.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Attaining the aim and remaining in good make successful functioning the test of that calibration.

## cosmic making to self cultivation
- reading: The person continues a cosmic sequence of making by cultivating the formed self as a small living world.
- mechanism: The preceding sequence presents illumination, construction, fertile extension, a living self, and its completion as coordinated making operations. The focus action becomes their human-scale continuation: the self is a made world whose viable ground must in turn be cultivated.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The tiller splitting ground supplies the human cultivation operation that answers the preceding acts of world-making.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase make the self's flourishing the product sought by that cultivation.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B002: Polishing and bringing to clarity contribute a preparatory operation that makes a formed thing effective and manifest.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Building by joining parts supplies a constructive mechanism rather than a merely decorative cosmic image.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: Soft, fertile, growth-bearing earth gives the cultivation model a receptive material substrate.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending provide the preparation of room in which cultivation can operate.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B011: The life-bearing self resolves the focus pronoun's near context and identifies the living field under cultivation.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion describe the self as formed capacity, ready for the distinct work of cultivation.

## revelation over concealment
- reading: Purification also uncovers and renders the soul's capacities legible, whereas failure hides them until their possible light cannot kindle.
- mechanism: Revelation, covering, diagnostic visibility, failed ignition, and secret insertion recast purification as bringing capacity into workable visibility. Cleaving opens; polishing discloses; concealment prevents the latent spark from becoming an achieved life.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving and opening supply the focus-side passage from closure to exposure.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude name the disclosed condition without reducing the operation to surface cleaning.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance establish manifestation as an active transformation.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering that rises over and hides something supplies the reversible counter-operation to disclosure.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The non-dominant mapped image of a visible sign by which a condition is recognized extends disclosure into diagnostic legibility.
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B002: A fire-striker that fails to kindle pictures latent capacity that never becomes operative.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion or hiding supplies the immediate anti-operation that keeps the self's contents unworked and unlit.

## governed thresholds not amputation
- reading: Purification can be read as skilled threshold-work: differentiating expansive drive, channeling it, and fitting protective limits so the whole self can grow.
- mechanism: The self is completed around a disclosed pair: expansive rupture and protective restraint. Successful cultivation differentiates these forces, opens channels for energy, and establishes safeguards; it need not mean amputating one half of the disclosure.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving makes discriminating channels and boundaries rather than indiscriminately destroying what is divided.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth makes viable increase, not mere suppression, the end of the internal regulation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B004: Fittingness contributes calibration of each capacity to an apt scope.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B006: A middle, equitable, or just position supplies the regulating geometry between live capacities.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Wide splitting and outburst contribute expansive force that can open a channel or exceed it.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding off harm supplies the protective boundary paired with expansive force.

## private cultivation to shared life
- reading: Self-cultivation proves itself publicly in bounded power, protection of shared sustenance, and refusal to disable another life for collective desire.
- mechanism: The later narrative externalizes inner regulation as collective handling of force and shared life-support: overflow becomes mobilized agency, contests a watering share, severs an animal's movement, and ends in eradication. The focus cultivation therefore acquires social and ecological fruit.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The ground-splitting tiller links personal success to stewardship of a field that sustains more than the isolated agent.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and thriving make preservation of shared life a visible fruit of self-cultivation.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Rising water and overwhelming force materialize transgression as energy that has exceeded its banks.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Stirring what was still into motion turns latent excess into an acting social agent.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An allotted share of watering and its channel supply the concrete common resource whose limit must be honored.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Hamstringing an animal's legs converts ungoverned force into the disabling of another living body's movement.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Total eradication supplies the terminal collapse opposite to growth and lasting success.

## fitted capacity not flattening
- reading: Successful fitting preserves a differentiated capacity to grow; equalization that merely flattens life belongs to the opposite pole.
- mechanism: The repeated equalizing root appears both in the self's formation and beside eradication. This makes equalization ambivalent: successful fitting must preserve differentiated life and growth, not merely make everything level.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B004: Suitability and fittingness retain calibration as a live focus operation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth supplies the criterion that distinguishes life-serving fit from sterile uniformity.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion present equalization first as constructive formation of the self.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Making things equal beside the destruction sequence introduces leveling as a potentially terminal operation.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication fixes the destructive pole against which mere leveling must be tested.

## success measured by what follows
- reading: The focus certifies a cultivation whose success is its maintained trajectory and the viable trace it leaves in later action.
- mechanism: The window opens a temporal pattern of one light following another and closes on aftermath, residue, and consequence. This activates the lasting-good branch of success: cultivation is judged by the trajectory and trace it leaves, not by an intense instant.
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B005: Success with continuance in good makes endurance intrinsic to the focus predicate.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth supplies an unfolding process whose truth appears over time.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession establish continuity from one phase into the next.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The end and consequence of a thing make aftermath an explicit horizon of evaluation.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remaining trace or residue asks what the cultivating action leaves behind.

## even pair without erasure
- reading: Purification makes a cleft but workable pair: expansive and protective capacities become distinguishable, proportioned, and governable.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B005: Pairing and evenness supply the odd but explicit root image of two terms held in relation.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B001: Cleaving supplies differentiation of the pair without requiring destruction of either member.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality or equivalence gives the two disclosed capacities a relational geometry.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Expansive rupture supplies one force in the context's explicitly coordinated pair.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Protective warding supplies the counter-force in that coordinated pair.

## nourished strength
- reading: Self-cultivation also feeds the strength needed for sustained growth at the right rhythm.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B006: The pre-dawn meal called falāḥ contributes timed nourishment that preserves strength for a continuing discipline.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase turn nourishment into a model of sustaining viable development.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B003: Forenoon food and early grazing echo nourishment at a marked phase of the daily cycle.

## certified against counterfeit success
- reading: The emphatic claim certifies only the win that produces real rectitude and growth, excluding manipulated or falsely advertised success.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B007: Sales puffing, false speech, and deception supply the possibility of success manufactured as an appearance.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B002: Purity and rectitude provide the focus-side criterion separating genuine success from its counterfeit.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B007: Gambling and victory by deception supply a contextual image of winning without durable good.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B001: Direct contradiction of truth makes counterfeit evaluation a consequential contextual act.


# Focus 91:10

## hidden self defeat
- reading: The failure is internally manufactured: the person disables access to the very self or capacity by which a sought good could be realized.
- mechanism: The sought good is not merely lost from outside. The agent makes the pronoun's referent unavailable, so the capacity through which a good could be sought or realized is itself hidden from use.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B001: The literal missed-object and deprived-fortune image supplies the result pole: effort reaches no benefit.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal covert insertion or concealment image supplies the operation that makes the referent inaccessible.

## corrupted no spark
- reading: Failure is a damaged conversion process: effort strikes, but a corrupted inner medium yields no living spark.
- mechanism: Corruption changes the medium, not just the verdict. Repeated effort can continue, yet nothing ignites because the inward material through which intention should become action has been spoiled.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B002: The literal fire-stick that gives no spark turns failure into an inability to convert striking effort into ignition.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal straying and spoiling image supplies the damage that explains why the inner medium no longer kindles.

## deprivation desiccation
- reading: Hiding it produces an impoverished, hungry, rainless interior whose powers can no longer be renewed.
- mechanism: What is hidden or corrupted is cut off from replenishment. The result is not only guilt but poverty of capacity: an interior that has lost its store, hunger that cannot be answered, or land that receives no rain.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B004: The literal poverty, loss, hunger, and rainless-land cluster supplies a depleted material state for failure.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal hiding image functions as withdrawal from the channels by which the interior could be exposed and replenished.

## false surface
- reading: The hidden reality permits a false exterior to take its place, so failure includes misrepresentation and self-misrecognition.
- mechanism: When the actual referent is pushed out of sight, a surface remains that can pass for reality. Failure then consists in living through an appearance that cannot deliver what its presentation promises.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B003: The literal vanity or falsehood image supplies an empty substitute that cannot fulfill its claim.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal concealment image places the real referent beneath the substitute surface.

## terminal cover versus rhythm
- reading: Cover can be legitimate and temporary; failure lies in turning it into a closed state that prevents renewed disclosure.
- mechanism: The context gives both emergence into light and legitimate nocturnal covering. This prevents a flat equation of all hiddenness with corruption: دَسَّىٰهَا becomes terminal or mistimed concealment, a cover that refuses the return to disclosure and therefore arrests the cycle by which hidden potential becomes visible.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal hiding operation remains the focus anchor whose duration and function are revised by the surrounding cycle.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: The literal emergence into the sun and appearance supplies the expected outward phase that terminal concealment blocks.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: The literal unveiling and becoming manifest makes disclosure an active operation rather than mere ambient light.
  - 91:4 **وَٱلَّيْلِ** ل ي ل B001: The literal darkness of night supplies a legitimate bounded interval of hiddenness.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: The literal cover laid over a thing shows that covering can serve a rhythm, sharpening the focus act as cover made one-way.

## anti cultivation
- reading: The decisive question is whether hidden potential can emerge and increase: failure is a corrupt burial that makes the inner ground sterile.
- mechanism: The earth, its spreading, the furrow-maker, and growth transform burial into an agronomic test. Hiding is not automatically sterile—a seed also enters the ground—but دَسَّىٰهَا is the handling that corrupts, compacts, or withholds conditions of emergence, while the adjacent alternative opens a furrow and tends increase.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B004: The literal rainless land and deprivation image supplies the sterile endpoint of failed cultivation.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal spoiling operation distinguishes corrupt burial from generative placement in soil.
  - 91:6 **وَٱلْأَرْضِ** ء ر ض B002: The literal soft fertile earth supplies a medium in which hiddenness can normally become growth.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: The literal spreading and extension supplies open room for development, the spatial opposite of suppressive compaction.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The literal plougher who cuts earth makes success an active technique of opening a growth channel.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: The literal increase and growth image supplies the observable output that differentiates cultivation from burial.

## whole self flattening
- reading: The person makes the whole formed self unavailable and flattened, rehearsing inwardly a loss of distinction that can later appear at collective scale.
- mechanism: The context identifies the recurring feminine referent with the self as a whole and brackets it between formation and later leveling or omission. دَسَّىٰهَا can therefore be read as self-erasure: hiding or corrupting the whole formed self until its distinctions are flattened, an inward small-scale analogue of the terminal leveling later applied collectively.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal concealment image supplies the inward act by which the formed self is made absent.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B012: The literal identity or very self of a thing makes the buried object a whole rather than one detachable appetite.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: The literal straightness and completion in the entity supplies the formed integrity that concealment deforms.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B011: The literal dropping or omission image activates a terminal flattening pole against the earlier completed form.

## covert ingress
- reading: The agent may also corrupt the self by admitting or planting something covertly inside it through a breached protective boundary.
- mechanism: The insertion side of د س و becomes active beside a breached covering and a protective barrier. The focus can describe not only pushing the self out of sight but also placing corruptive material secretly into it through a failed boundary.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal hidden insertion image supplies covert inward entry as an alternative direction for the focus operation.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal spoiling image identifies the functional effect of what is covertly inserted.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: The literal deviation and breach of a covering supplies the compromised boundary through which hidden corruption can enter.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: The literal warding of harm supplies the protective function whose failure permits covert ingress.

## deceptive garment
- reading: Burial generates a false public surface, so the failure includes maintaining an appearance that conceals and misstates the self beneath it.
- mechanism: The later denial activates a garment-like false exterior. Concealment does not leave a neutral blank; it enables a presentation that lies about the condition beneath it, turning inward suppression into public misrepresentation.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B003: The literal vanity or falsehood image supplies the empty claim whose promised result fails.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal concealment image places the actual inner condition beneath an exterior.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B009: The literal garment that misreports its wearer's condition gives the concealed self a deceptive social surface.

## latent pressure proxy
- reading: A group may bury a shared disposition until it overflows through an activated representative, while personal agency remains live within the collective mechanism.
- mechanism: Inward suppression and outward excess become opposite phases of one latent system. What a group keeps hidden can accumulate like overpassing water, be stirred from rest, and emerge through a single maximally wretched agent; the focus subject can thus be carried at both personal and collective scales.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal corruption image supplies the latent disposition stored within the person or group.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: The literal water rising beyond its limit supplies outward overflow as the release phase of suppressed corruption.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: The literal stirring of something stationary supplies the transition from latent condition to activated conduct.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: The literal wretchedness branch supplies the human proxy in whom the group's buried condition becomes concentrated and visible.

## suppressed message and share
- reading: The failure can consist in burying a limit already known inwardly and stated publicly, especially when that limit governs another's share.
- mechanism: Speech, message, and an allotted channel of water make the buried content more specific: it can be an already recognized claim that is kept from governing conduct. Failure is then not simple ignorance but suppression of an inwardly apprehended and publicly articulated limit.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal concealment image supplies the suppression of a claim that has reached awareness.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B012: The literal saying held within and not manifested supplies an internal acknowledgment that can be buried rather than enacted.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: The literal messenger and message externalize the claim, removing lack of communication as the only explanation.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The literal share of watering and its channel gives the communicated limit a concrete resource-allocation function.

## root cutting scale
- reading: Inner spoiling tends toward cutting the roots of shared life, replacing nourishment and growth with barrenness and eventual extirpation.
- mechanism: The later sequence scales inner spoiling into destruction of generative infrastructure: the root is cut, the medium becomes barren, and extirpation follows, opposite a branch of nourishment and growth. دَسَّىٰهَا is therefore a small-scale beginning of root-destruction, not a condition that remains safely hidden.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B004: The literal rainless deprivation image supplies sterility as the focus-level result.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal spoiling image supplies the inward first stage of the destructive progression.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B013: The literal root or base of a thing turns the violent act into removal of generative support.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B017: The literal barren sand that does not grow supplies the ecological output of root-cutting.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: The literal extirpation image supplies the collective terminal scale of the process.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant mapped branch's literal nourishment and growth image supplies the live counter-mechanism to barrenness and extirpation.

## delayed aftermath
- reading: The agent may seem successful and feel no alarm in the short term; failure is disclosed by the tail, remainder, and aftermath that concealment cannot erase.
- mechanism: Tail, end, expected harm, aftermath, and remainder make failure longitudinal. Concealment can appear to erase a cause and may produce no immediate alarm, but what is hidden leaves a trailing effect by which the verdict خَابَ is eventually disclosed.
- trace:
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B001: The literal missing of the sought result supplies failure as an outcome measured beyond the immediate act.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal concealment image supplies the apparent disappearance of the cause before its effects return.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The literal tail or end of a thing supplies a consequence that remains attached behind the concealed act.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: The literal dread that anticipates harm supplies the subjective alarm whose absence need not cancel objective failure.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The literal end and aftermath of a thing supplies the later horizon on which failure becomes legible.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: The literal remainder and trace supplies evidence that burial never fully removes what was done.

## aperture and poison
- reading: Corruption may begin as minute covert ingress through an unguarded aperture and spoil the self from within before concealment is noticed.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal hidden insertion image anchors the direction of covert entry.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal spoiling image anchors the harmful transformation caused after entry.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: The non-dominant branch's literal narrow aperture supplies the small, easily missed route of ingress.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B002: The non-dominant branch's literal poison entering a body supplies the cross-domain model of inward corruption.

## undigested inspiration
- reading: What enters inwardly must be discriminated and integrated; swallowing it whole and leaving it hidden can turn reception into an undigested source of corruption.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: The literal hidden insertion image anchors inward reception that remains unexposed and unworked.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal spoiling image supplies the risk when received material is not differentiated or transformed.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: The literal swallowing and taking in completely supplies an ingestion model for inward reception.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: The literal placing of oneself within protection supplies the selective boundary needed for safe inward reception.

## propagated burial
