- reading: The corrupted pattern can acquire followers, propagate by imitation, and leave a social tail beyond the initiating self.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: The literal leading-astray and spoiling image anchors an operation that can be done to another and therefore copied socially.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B012: The non-dominant branch's literal following in error supplies transmission by imitation along a sequence.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B003: The literal followers or tails image supplies the social remainder that trails and reproduces an initiating corruption.


# Focus 91:11

## collective falsification
- reading: Thamud collectively pronounced truth false, with its own limit-breaking condition functioning as both pressure behind and vehicle of the pronouncement.
- mechanism: The collective does more than hold an incorrect belief: it issues a verdict that marks a claim or claimant false. The prefixed bi-phrase can carry cause or instrument, so boundary-crossing is both a motive for the verdict and a medium through which it is enforced.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring a report or its bearer false supplies the core collective verdict.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Overstepping a proper bound in rebellion supplies the cause and manner of that verdict.

## failed charge
- reading: Denial is also conduct that makes an entrusted charge come out false by refusing to carry it through within its bounds.
- mechanism: Truth and falsehood can be tested by whether an undertaking is carried through. Joined to transgressive overflow, denial can therefore name a failed charge or betrayed obligation, not speech alone.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B004: A charge that proves false by not being carried through supplies a performance test for denial.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Rebellious boundary-breaking supplies the failure condition for the entrusted charge.

## denial as overflow
- reading: The cause is a surging collective force that overruns a boundary and gives a false verdict practical dominance.
- mechanism: The verdict of falsehood is driven by a flood-like social force. Once the collective rises beyond a boundary, denial becomes an overwhelming pressure that can make falsehood operative even without changing what is true.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing falsehood supplies the judgment imposed by the collective.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Water or force surging beyond measure supplies a physical model for how the judgment becomes socially overwhelming.

## revelation cover cycle
- reading: Thamud's denial actively re-covered a truth that the surrounding sequence imagines as repeatedly brought into view.
- mechanism: Emergence into visibility and active unveiling alternate with covering. Against that cycle, the focus denial reads as a cover repeatedly laid over what repeatedly becomes visible, not as a one-time absence of evidence.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring something false supplies the epistemic intervention made by Thamud.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B002: Coming out into sunlight supplies an initial movement from concealment to exposure.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and manifestation sharpen exposure into active disclosure.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering laid over and hiding something supplies the counter-movement performed by denial.

## trailing claim
- reading: The denied claim remains attached as a trailing liability and eventually returns as the event's residue and consequence.
- mechanism: A right or liability can follow its bearer, while an aftermath remains as a thing's tail or trace. The collective verdict does not erase the denied claim; it converts the claim into something that follows the deniers and becomes consequence.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: The attempt to mark a claim false supplies the act that tries to cancel its standing.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B004: A right or liability following its owner supplies the persistence of what denial cannot discharge.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remainder or trace supplies the eventual form taken by the still-following claim.

## breached architecture
- reading: Transgression is a structural breach of fitted limits, and denial is one way the communal architecture ceases to carry its charge.
- mechanism: Building by joined parts, spreading a stable field, and proportioning a self establish an architecture of fitted relations. Transgression is then not vague wickedness but load, reach, or force passing the limits that let the architecture hold; denial is its cognitive and social breach.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Overstepping a proper bound supplies the breach of an ordered construction.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B004: Failure to carry a charge through supplies the behavioral failure produced by that breach.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Construction by joining parts supplies the load-bearing order that excess violates.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supplies the bounded field in which communal action occurs.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion within a thing supply the fitted self that transgression deforms.

## internal rupture over guard
- reading: Its excess is also the enacted rupture of a protection already available within the self, with denial externalizing that refused guard.
- mechanism: The paired possibilities inside the self become two material operations: a wide rupture that breaches a cover and a protection that wards damage. The focus transgression selects and amplifies the rupture; the outward verdict of falsehood is the social expression of an inwardly available but refused guard.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Boundary-crossing supplies the focus-side realization of the inner breach.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring false supplies the outward judgment generated by the selected inner path.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Taking something in wholly supplies a material analogy for alternatives becoming inwardly available, not a replacement gloss for the contextual verb.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A wide split and bursting-out supply the mechanics of a breached inner boundary.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Deviation from right and tearing a cover connect the physical rupture to moral action.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding off damage supplies the live protective alternative that the rupture defeats.

## cultivation or burial
- reading: Denial is active burial of recognized truth, the inverse of opening the self's ground for growth.
- mechanism: One treatment of ground opens it for growth; the opposed treatment hides something inside it and ends in failed pursuit. This converts denial from lack of recognition into anti-cultivation: recognized truth is pressed below the surface so that transgressive desire can occupy the visible field.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring false supplies the public surface placed over what is suppressed.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: A cultivator splitting the earth supplies productive opening of the inner ground.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the result of opening that ground well.
  - 91:10 **خَابَ** خ ي ب B001: Missing the sought end supplies the eventual failure of concealment.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion or burial supplies the active suppression beneath denial's surface.

## catalytic agent collective
- reading: The worst member is the ignition point through which the community's already shared transgression becomes collective action.
- mechanism: A dormant force is stirred into motion through the community's most wretched member. The individual does not replace collective agency; he concentrates and releases a regime already named as Thamud's own transgressive falsification.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: The collective verdict of falsehood keeps responsibility at the communal level.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B003: A head of misguidance supplies the social form in which collective excess can become concentrated.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Stirring something stationary from rest supplies the release of latent communal force.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposed to flourishing marks the selected agent as the community's concentrated negative pole.

## embodied water right
- reading: Thamud enacts denial by sabotaging an embodied sign and the bounded distribution of life-giving water that the sign protects.
- mechanism: The warning is carried by a messenger but also embodied in an animal and a defined share or channel of water. The immediate sequence from repeated denial to disabling the animal makes falsification performative: the community nullifies a sign by attacking the living arrangement that lets it signify.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B004: A charge proving false through failed performance opens the focus to later enacted denial.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's own indication counting as speech lets the protected arrangement carry an argument materially.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply the explicit warning whose truth is at stake.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The concrete camel image supplies the living bearer of the sign and obligation.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of watering and its channel supply the bounded common resource the warning protects.
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B004: The repeated failed-charge image turns the warning into a test immediately answered by conduct.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs supplies the concrete disabling act by which the community makes its verdict operative.

## overflow reversed by leveling
- reading: The community's overrun is a force-pattern that the context later reverses through eradication and leveling, so its excess already carries the shape of its undoing.
- mechanism: The focus root can image both an over-bound surge and an overwhelming punitive force. Later eradication under sovereign rule and equalization reverse the community's self-elevating overflow: the force that exceeded measure is brought to an undifferentiated level through the consequence attached to its guilt.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: A rising, sweeping force supplies the geometry of the community's transgression.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B004: An overwhelming punishment supplies a latent reversal within the same focus-root inventory.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the terminal counterforce to the prior surge.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Sovereignty and mastery identify the governing power that contains the unbounded force.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B001: Guilt supplies the causal attachment between communal action and its reversal.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalizing two things supplies the leveling endpoint opposed to self-elevating excess.

## feedbackless aftermath
- reading: Denial also disables anticipation of the warning's tail, making transgression a system that refuses corrective feedback; this remains a framing contrast if the closing subject is not Thamud.
- mechanism: Fear anticipates harm, and an aftermath is the tail left by an act. The closing negation lets the focus transgression be read as a broken feedback system: the collective verdict severs present desire from the future trace that should restrain it.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing a proper limit supplies action that has ceased to regulate itself.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring the warning false supplies the cognitive cut that disables restraint.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Alarm that anticipates coming harm supplies the missing forward-looking feedback.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The end and outcome of a thing supply the future state that denial refuses to admit.

## acoustic overrun
- reading: Their transgression can be heard as collective noise that performs denial by drowning the warning and swelling into action.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring a speaker or report false supplies the hostile verdict carried by the sound.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B008: A people's voice or noise supplies the unusual acoustic body of their excess.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Spoken utterance supplies the message that communal noise can overwhelm.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B009: Raising the voice supplies a bridge from acoustic opposition to the violent action that follows.

## failed sustaining yield
- reading: Thamud makes a promised sustaining arrangement fail to endure, materially producing the falsity it asserts.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B006: Milk disappearing or failing to last as expected supplies the latent image of broken sustenance.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The camel supplies the concrete animal body that makes the yield image newly salient.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A watering share and channel supply the expected continuity of common provision.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Disabling the animal's legs supplies the act that makes the sustaining arrangement fail.

## deceptive surface
- reading: Denial is also a manufactured public surface that lies by how it looks, covering what recurring disclosure would otherwise reveal.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B009: A patterned or dyed surface that deceives by its condition supplies denial as fabricated appearance.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B002: Polishing and bringing clarity supply the exposure of what lies beneath the deceptive surface.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A cover laid over something supplies the obscuring layer from which the deceptive surface is made.


# Focus 91:12

## kinetic extreme
- reading: When the collective's wretched extreme turned from a rank within it into its advancing edge.
- mechanism: The subject is first a ranked quality within a group and then becomes its moving edge: the collective's wretched extreme converts from latent status into self-propelled action.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: Setting out and pressing onward gives the event a self-propelled kinetic onset.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposed to happiness supplies the qualitative extreme carried by the moving subject.

## roused hardship
- reading: A dormant, hardship-formed capacity was roused into consequential motion.
- mechanism: The event is activation rather than mere standing: something dormant or restrained is stirred, and what is stirred is the member or capacity most formed by difficulty and struggle.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing a still or restrained thing supplies the transition from latency to agitation.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B002: Hardship, fatigue, and strenuous handling make the activated extreme hardship-formed rather than merely morally labeled.

## dispatched contestant
- reading: A hardship-contest extreme set out as though directed toward a role or task.
- mechanism: The focus alone permits a social-role possibility: the extreme actor is not just impulsive but resembles a contestant who has prevailed in harsh struggle and now goes out as a task-directed agent.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B002: Dispatch and direction toward a task keep a mission-shaped reading available beneath the intransitive form.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B003: Prevailing in a hardship contest recasts the superlative as a perverse champion or selected extreme.

## concealment to eruption
- reading: The collective's most hardship-laden capacity surfaced from concealment as a pressure release.
- mechanism: The reveal-cover alternation, followed by images of splitting and hidden insertion, turns the focus activation into a pressure event: the collective's most hardship-laden material breaks cover and becomes visible motion.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing from stillness supplies the latent-to-active transition at the center of the mechanism.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B002: Strenuous hardship identifies what has accumulated beneath the surface and is released.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Disclosure and appearance make emergence readable as becoming exposed.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering laid over something supplies the prior state of occlusion.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: Wide splitting and bursting give the transition a pressure-break geometry.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion or burial supplies the hidden location from which the activated extreme surfaces.

## collective counter envoy
- reading: The group's extreme became its counter-envoy, answering a spoken warning with an enacted rival message.
- mechanism: Collective denial, the focus dispatch echo, and the immediately following messenger form two opposed missions. The singular extreme can therefore be read as the group's embodied counter-message, socially representative even if no explicit commissioning is stated.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B002: Dispatch toward a task makes the focus actor available as a mission-bearing agent.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B003: Prevailing in harsh contention supplies the social profile of a champion selected by escalation.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing a person or claim to falsehood supplies the collective stance that the singular actor comes to embody.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Overt utterance makes the messenger's mission explicit and sharpens its contrast with the focus actor's enacted message.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B001: Sending and setting forth mirrors the focus dispatch branch and produces rival mobilizations.

## protected share breach
- reading: The collective's extreme rose to convert its excess into a breach of protected, allotted access.
- mechanism: Protection, limit-crossing, the named camel, and an allotted watering share turn the focus motion into an assault on a bounded common. The extreme actor is the point where collective excess becomes interference with protected access and flow.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: Pressing onward supplies the directed intervention that crosses into the protected arrangement.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness marks the actor as the collective's destructive extreme within this allocation conflict.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding harm through protection supplies the idea of a boundary meant to preserve what is vulnerable.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing a limit in disobedience identifies the collective force that the extreme actor converts into action.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The concrete she-camel gives the protected arrangement a living material bearer.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An allotted share of watering and its channel turn the warning into a rule of distribution and access.

## mobility reversal
- reading: His acquired mobility became the means by which the protected animal was deprived of mobility.
- mechanism: The focus gives mobility to one body, then the narrative names a camel and removes the animal's locomotion. The actor's rising is thus not generic readiness but the kinetic precondition for transferring stillness and disablement onto another body.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing a kneeling or restrained creature gives the focus motion a specifically animal and bodily edge.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The she-camel's concrete body supplies the counterpart onto which arrested motion will be imposed.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs realizes the deliberate destruction of stance and locomotion.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B003: Damage to the back and movement broadens the result from injury to systematic immobilization.

## extreme between levelings
- reading: An extreme deviation projected from a proportioned whole and precipitated the re-leveling of the entire field.
- mechanism: A proportioned whole emits its superlative outlier; after that outlier acts, uprooting and the return of the same equalizing root erase the differential. The focus becomes an extreme point suspended between formation and re-leveling.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness, intensified by the superlative form, supplies the extreme point within the collective.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: Outward pressing turns the extreme from a position in the field into a protruding event.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and adjustment frame the prior whole from which the superlative member emerges.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Complete uprooting removes both the activated protrusion and the collective structure sustaining it.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: The returning equalization image supplies the endpoint that abolishes the differential.

## action grows a tail
- reading: The rising launched an act with a tail: collective follow-through, destruction, and an enduring aftermath.
- mechanism: Tail, anticipated harm, and final consequence extend the focus beyond a punctual outburst. The rising initiates an event that acquires a trailing collective sequel and terminates in an aftermath no higher agent fears.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: Setting out supplies the first forward step in the consequential chain.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The tail or terminal part of a thing gives the wrongdoing an attached sequel rather than an isolated instant.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fear as expectation of coming harm makes futurity explicit even under negation.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The last part and consequence name the terminal horizon toward which the focus action travels.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remainder or trace preserves the idea that the act leaves something following behind it.

## inner extreme takes agency
- reading: Alongside the historical actor, the line models the instant a self or collective lets its most self-destructive capacity take agency.
- mechanism: The soul sequence permits an intrapsychic analogue: a self receives moral material, can hide it, and then its most self-destructive capacity becomes an acting subject. The historical collective and an interior economy remain co-present rather than being collapsed.
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Waking a dormant thing lets an inner disposition cross into agency.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness supplies the destructive pole personified as the self's extreme.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B013: What lies within the self as reason or inward alarm supplies an interior arena of response.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B014: Strength and disposition of the self allow one latent capacity to become dominant.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Swallowing and fully taking in something gives inspiration an embodied inward-incorporation image.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Hidden insertion or burial supplies the mechanism by which a destructive capacity remains latent before rising.

## rising spur then level field
- reading: A ridge-like social extreme thrust out from the field and was finally leveled back into it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B004: Pressing onward lets the social extreme appear spatially as a projection from a field.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B004: The long rising, climbable mountain flank supplies the outlier's ridge-like shape.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: Height and elevation establish the vertical axis on which the protrusion rises.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extension supply the broad field against which the ridge stands out.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Broad smooth ground supplies the terminal image of the protruding extreme flattened back into the field.

## perverse champion selection
- reading: Competitive escalation produced a perverse champion, and the collective deployed its winner.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B003: Prevailing in a hardship contest supplies the perverse champion selected from the collective.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B002: Dispatch toward a task turns selection into deployment.
  - 91:2 **وَٱلْقَمَرِ** ق م ر B007: Gambling and prevailing through deception supply a remote model of winner-producing risk.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B007: Rivalry and trying to outdo another provide the escalation that ranks one member above the rest.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B011: Mutual vaunting and abuse carry the contest mechanism into hostile group performance.

## refractory animal double
- reading: A refractory human double was released from restraint and asserted its freedom by destroying the governed animal's freedom of movement.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:1 **وَٱلشَّمْسِ** ش م س B002: Refractoriness in animal or character imports a balking, ungoverned temperament from a distant opening branch.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing a kneeling or restrained creature makes the actor's release bodily and animal-like.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B004: Taming, training, and management position the camel as the governed counterpart to the refractory actor.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Hamstringing destroys the governed counterpart's capacity to stand and move.


# Focus 91:13

## protected life and share
- reading: The utterance establishes one protected life-support unit: violating either the she-camel or her allocated water-access violates the announced divine charge.
- mechanism: An authorized, articulated message places a living creature and her allocated access to water inside one announced divine boundary. The warning protects not only an animal as property but the life-support entitlement attached to her.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Articulated speech supplies the public speech-act that makes the protected boundary audible.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and carried message make the speaker's authority inseparable from the warning he transmits.
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B002: The fixed divine name makes the two genitives a repeated seal over messenger and camel.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The literal she-camel supplies the vulnerable living bearer of the protected claim.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An irrigation share and its channel turn watering into an apportioned right rather than an incidental drink.

## stewardship obligation
- reading: Accept an ongoing stewardship duty: manage conduct and shared water so the divinely assigned animal can actually receive her provision.
- mechanism: Branches of earnest concern, deliberate conduct, animal management, and provision shift the sentence from a negative taboo to a positive duty of stewardship. The hearers must actively make the shared setting workable for the animal.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B015: Earnest concern makes the saying an act of committed care for what is named.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B004: Gentleness and deliberate pace supply a non-impulsive mode for administering the warning.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B004: Taming, training, and managing make the camel a charge requiring competent social handling.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B002: Providing a watering source supplies the positive obligation to preserve access, not merely to refrain from harm.

## recurrent turn
- reading: Preserve the camel's recurrent turn in a shared sequence; the protected object is a schedule as much as a quantity of water.
- mechanism: Succession and following branches activate a schedule rather than a one-time event. The warning protects recurrence: the camel must arrive in her turn, and the community must let the sequence continue.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B005: Succession in batches supplies an ordered procession of users or turns.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The literal she-camel identifies the participant whose place in the sequence is at issue.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The irrigation-share branch supplies the recurring allotment that needs an order.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B012: The non-dominant mapped branch of following one after another gives the allotment a procession-like temporal form.

## embodied message
- reading: The words install an embodied message: the camel's unhindered drinking, or its obstruction, visibly states the community's answer.
- mechanism: A branch in which a thing's 'saying' is its indication allows the animal's visible treatment to carry the message after the messenger speaks. The community answers the speech by what it lets the camel's body do at the water.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's indication as its saying lets material conduct become legible speech.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message establish the verbal transmission that the material arrangement can enact.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The camel's visible body supplies the sign-bearing participant.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B001: Actual giving of drink makes compliance or refusal publicly observable.

## reciprocal flow
- reading: Watering sustains a reciprocal living flow; the warning opposes an extractive order that wants yield while severing the creature's required inflow.
- mechanism: The messenger root's branch of milk and continuous yield activates a metabolic circuit around the camel: water enters a living animal and sustaining yield can flow outward. Protection concerns continuity of exchange, not possession of an inert object.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B006: Milk and continuous animal yield supply the outward-flowing side of a reciprocal resource circuit.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The she-camel supplies the living converter between incoming water and possible outward yield.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies the necessary inflow that keeps the living circuit open.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B015: Earnest concern makes continuity of the circuit the object of the messenger's care.

## cosmic turn order
- reading: Her turn participates in the same kind of ordered alternation displayed by the cosmic sequence; blocking it is an attempt to monopolize a cycle that works through succession.
- mechanism: Extension, following, disclosure, and covering form an alternation of timed relations before the focus. They strengthen the baseline water-share into a turn nested within an ordered cycle: a rightful participant appears, receives its interval, and yields to what follows.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The focus branch supplies the apportioned water-share whose temporal form is being revised.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B005: Succession in batches supplies a focus-local seed for recurrent turns.
  - 91:1 **وَضُحَىٰهَا** ض ح و B001: Daylight's extension supplies an interval that opens and occupies time.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and succession make one participant's place depend on ordered relation to another.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Disclosure gives each turn a phase of public appearance rather than secret possession.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering that succeeds disclosure supplies closure and handoff to the cycle.

## habitat load bearing share
- reading: Their obligation tests whether their built and spread habitat can include a nonhuman claimant; the water-share functions as civic infrastructure, not private indulgence.
- mechanism: Building by joining parts and spreading a traversable ground recast the water allotment as habitat architecture. The camel's share is not a detachable favor: it is one relation whose removal makes the inhabited order fail its own construction.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B004: Animal management supplies the concrete stewardship problem inside the habitat.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The irrigation share supplies the load-bearing allocation within the shared setting.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Construction by joining parts supplies the model of an order held together by relations.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supplies the common ground across which the managed allocation operates.

## other self and equalization
- reading: The rule asks whether communal balance can recognize another embodied self; refusal converts a gentle proportional measure into an indiscriminate equalization.
- mechanism: The living self and the repeated act of making even bring the camel's embodied need into the surah's balance language. The same root that accompanies the self's formation later accompanies the aftermath, so the focus becomes an offered equilibrium whose rejection returns as a harsher equalization.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The literal animal anchors the reading in an embodied other rather than an abstract moral theme.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: An apportioned share supplies the practical measure by which balance can include that other.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B011: The life-bearing self supplies a category in which bodily dependence matters morally.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and commensuration supply the offered ordering of competing claims.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: The repeated equality branch makes the later outcome a severe return of the same ordering vocabulary.

## spoken moral fork
- reading: The warning gives the audience a material fork: preserve a boundary and enact protection, or pierce it and enact the rupture already available within the self.
- mechanism: Ingestion, rupture, and protective self-placement turn the messenger's external words into a concrete form of the inward fork. Respecting the water boundary performs protection; breaking through it performs rupture. The camel makes an otherwise interior distinction materially decidable.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Articulated speech supplies the external statement that can be received or resisted.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The bounded water-share supplies the concrete line across which either restraint or rupture is enacted.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Swallowing and taking something fully inside supply a material image for inward reception.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Deviation and breaching a cover supply the destructive option of crossing the announced limit.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Placing oneself within protection supplies the alternative of restraint around the vulnerable claim.

## cultivation or burial
- reading: The request tests their model of prosperity: genuine cultivation makes room for another life, whereas apparent gain buries the limiting claim that exposes extraction.
- mechanism: Ploughing, growth, and hidden insertion activate an ecological and psychic reading of the water-share. Shared water can cultivate life, but a community can also bury the claim that constrains its own land use. The camel becomes a rival claimant through whom cultivation is distinguished from concealed extraction.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The irrigation share supplies the contested ecological resource linking land, growth, and animal life.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The farmer who cuts the earth supplies the productive land-use pressure around shared water.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the life-enhancing result that proper watering can serve.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion or burial supplies the contrary move of suppressing an inconvenient claim.

## transgression as overflow
- reading: Their transgression takes the rule's own material form: they first deny the boundary and then overflow the share, turning a managed current into unbounded appropriation.
- mechanism: Attribution to falsehood removes the warning's truth-status, while the split transgression inventory supplies both crossing a limit and water rising beyond bounds. Because the focus boundary is itself a water allotment, moral transgression and hydraulic overflow become structurally homologous.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: The messenger's articulated saying supplies the truth-claim that the group can reject.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A water-share and channel supply the bounded flow whose limit must hold.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing a speaker or thing to falsehood supplies the discursive removal of the boundary's authority.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Exceeding a limit in disobedience supplies the social form of transgression.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Water overflowing its bound supplies the material analogue that makes violation of a water-share especially charged.

## counter dispatch
- reading: The saying is a last directional intervention: one dispatched figure uses speech to arrest the destructive trajectory of another activated figure.
- mechanism: The sent messenger and the roused, struggling wrongdoer become opposed vectors. The saying is an interceptive act inserted into motion: authorized dispatch seeks to slow and redirect a destructive self-launch before it reaches the camel.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B001: Being sent forth supplies the authorized vector embodied by the messenger.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Articulated speech supplies the braking intervention made within the unfolding action.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing what was still supplies the antagonist's abrupt launch into action.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B003: Struggling through hardship supplies the actor's grim persistence once mobilized.

## body and support attack
- reading: It names an integrated vulnerability in advance: once the message is denied, the body that bears the claim is disabled and its access to sustaining flow is rendered impossible.
- mechanism: The later sequence moves from repudiating the message to cutting the animal's legs. It confirms that the focus pair protects an organism together with its sustaining access: discursive denial clears the way for an attack on the bodily carrier of the water claim.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The literal she-camel supplies the body named in the protected pair.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies the bodily support process coordinated with the animal.
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing the messenger to falsehood supplies the discursive act that deauthorizes his protection.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting an animal's legs supplies the literal destruction of its mobility and bodily support.

## nurture to leveling reversal
- reading: Divine ownership first appears as maintenance of a vulnerable share; punishment is the catastrophic reversal in which the order that refused measured provision loses its own differentiated stability.
- mechanism: Nurturing completion, extirpation, and equalization create a severe reversal around the repeated divine relation. The focus's protected provision can be read as maintenance offered by the divine owner; after its rejection, the same ordering power no longer maintains differentiated shares but levels the order that destroyed them.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B002: The repeated fixed divine name supplies the ownership relation that links warning and consequence.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B002: Provision of a watering source supplies the maintenance mode offered in the focus.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the terminal mode that replaces failed maintenance.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, and completion make lordship legible first as ordering care rather than mere domination.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization supplies the reversal from differentiated allotment to a flattened outcome.

## prospective aftermath
- reading: He exposes the causal hinge while choice remains: preserving one bounded water relation averts the long tail, whereas crossing it releases an aftermath no later deliberation can recall.
- mechanism: Tail, anticipated harm, and aftermath turn the focus speech into a prospective causal break-point. The messenger names the vulnerable relation before the action so that the hearers can prevent the tail of consequences; the later outcome is not external to the warning but what the warning was trying to interrupt.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Articulated speech supplies the timely intervention before consequence becomes irreversible.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The water-share supplies the small present boundary at which the future chain can still be stopped.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The tail or end of a thing supplies a consequence that trails the initiating act.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fear as anticipation of harm supplies the prospective stance available to the human audience.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The end and aftermath of a thing supply the terminal state already latent in the warned-against act.

## anatomy of support
- reading: A form-distant anatomy gathers around the phrase: external flow and bodily support are one vulnerability, later cut at the legs and preserved only as an aftermath-trace.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The non-dominant shank and supporting-stem branch overlays watering with the structure that carries a body.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The literal she-camel supplies the body whose support and movement are at stake.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting the animal's legs realizes the destruction of bodily support.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B002: Heel and trace extend the support image into what remains behind after movement is stopped.

## command as forced drink
- reading: His saying also performs a figurative watering of the hearers: it makes them take in a disliked limit, and their denial is a refusal of that inward draught before they deny the camel's literal one.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B001: Articulated speech supplies what is being pressed upon the audience for reception.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B010: Making a heart drink what it dislikes turns the water warning into an unwelcome verbal dose.
  - 91:8 **فَأَلْهَمَهَا** ل ه م B001: Swallowing something fully supplies the inward path by which the spoken limit could be received.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing the message to falsehood supplies the refusal to ingest or retain the unwelcome command.

## hydraulic tail of guilt
- reading: The violated water boundary generates its own downstream tail: guilt and aftermath appear as the channel-end and residue of the first act of appropriation.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The irrigation share and channel supply the upstream allocation from which consequences can flow.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B004: Watercourse tails and valley ends give the fault a spatially downstream image.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remnant and trace supply what the initiating violation leaves behind.

## measured vessel network
- reading: A remote vessel-and-portion network asks whether access also entails measurable volume: jar, draught, share, and full bucket shadow the politics of how much each claimant receives.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B003: The non-dominant large water-jar branch gives the utterance a remote volumetric container image.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: The irrigation share supplies the actual measured entitlement that controls the vessel network.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B006: A draught or portion of drink supplies the small embodied unit of consumption.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B007: A full bucket supplies the drawn and bounded quantity at the far end of the sequence.

## water to yield circuit
- reading: The camel is a living circulation point: water received can become growth and yield, so severing her access destroys reciprocity and reveals a community seeking output without sustaining input.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B006: Milk and continuous livestock yield supply the outward flow enabled by care of the animal.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The literal she-camel supplies the living middle of the input-output circuit.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B001: Giving drink supplies the incoming flow without which the living yield cannot continue.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the generative effect of maintaining the circuit.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B005: Feeding and development supply the nurturing process that connects inflow to living increase.


# Focus 91:14

## culpability chain
- reading: The verse stages a self-tightening sequence in which a social verdict authorizes violence, violence fixes culpability, and sovereign requital equalizes the whole implicated field.
- mechanism: A public verdict of falsehood licenses an embodied attack; the attack establishes culpability; sovereign eradication answers that culpability; the final act removes distinctions across the affected collective or site. The verse is thus a compressed causal mechanism rather than a list of adjacent events.
- trace:
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B002: The branch contributes attribution of falsehood and makes the opening verb a public delegitimating judgment that enables what follows.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: The branch contributes cutting an animal down by disabling its legs, converting the prior judgment into irreversible bodily action.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Complete eradication supplies the totalizing force of the answering event.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Lordship, ownership, and authority identify the answering power as one entitled to govern the violated order.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B001: Culpable offense supplies the explicit causal hinge between the communal act and its consequence.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equality and equivalence make the endpoint a leveling that removes prior differences among the affected.

## attached tail
- reading: Their act carries a tail of consequence within it: the attempt to make the warning fail runs through the attack to an eradication that completes the deed's own trajectory.
- mechanism: The community tries to make the warning fail by carrying its assault through, but the deed acquires a trailing consequence. Hamstringing something rare or final without successor is answered by eradication, and the tail of the act reaches a completed endpoint.
- trace:
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B004: The branch tests whether a charge proves true by being carried through or false by failing, casting denial as an attempted practical falsification.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B005: The image of a final rare thing with no successor gives the attack a finality extending beyond the immediate wound.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: The physical tail or rear end supplies the image of a consequence that remains attached behind the initiating act.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication is the terminal event reached by the deed's attached consequence.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Straightness and completed form close the sequence rather than leaving its consequence unfinished.

## counter formation
- reading: The response mirrors the deeper structure of the offense: those who cut a source of continuation encounter the cultivator's destructive counter-formation of their own collective order.
- mechanism: The community attacks a life-bearing source and interrupts continuation. The answering Lord is activated not only as sovereign but as cultivator: eradication and leveling become a severe counter-formation of an order whose capacity for growth has been deliberately cut.
- trace:
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B007: Cutting a palm's crown or a bird's regrowth source makes the attack an interruption of future growth, not only a present wound.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Repair, nurture, administration, and stagewise completion activate the Lord as the one who forms and maintains an order.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant mapped inventory contributes nourishment and growing up, sharpening the contrast between cultivated life and its deliberate cutting.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Complete eradication scales the community's interruption of life up to the social whole.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Sound completion makes the final leveling an imposed re-formation, though the resulting form is destructive for the offenders.

## exposure after cover
- reading: Denial sustains a false covering; the attack discloses what the covering held, and the answering leveling abolishes the deceptive surface itself.
- mechanism: The preceding reveal-cover alternation turns denial into the maintenance of a false surface. The embodied attack makes that concealed stance visible, while total eradication and the final open-ground image remove both cover and deceptive differentiation.
- trace:
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Disclosure and appearance provide the revealing pole of the mechanism.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A cover rising over and concealing something provides the opposed pole of hiddenness.
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B009: A patterned surface that deceives by its appearance lets denial operate as a socially maintained false display.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication forcibly ends the concealed arrangement once action has exposed it.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Wide smooth ground supplies the endpoint as a surface on which no deceptive relief remains.

## forming reversal
- reading: The final leveling is the formal inverse of earlier creation: eradication is followed by a deliberate return of the differentiated order to evenness.
- mechanism: Building, spreading, individuating a self, and making it sound form an ordered construction sequence. The focus repeats the making-even root after eradication, so its last act reads as deliberate reverse construction: differentiated life and settlement are returned to an imposed even state.
- trace:
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Building by joining parts supplies the constructive starting operation.
  - 91:6 **طَحَىٰهَا** ط ح و B001: Spreading and extending supplies the preparation of an inhabitable field.
  - 91:7 **وَنَفْسٍ** ن ف س B012: The very self or identity supplies the individuated object brought into form.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Straightness and sound completion establish making-even as a formative operation earlier in the sequence.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B002: Nurture, administration, and completion identify the actor as one who can form and reform an order.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication supplies the destructive phase separating original formation from reverse formation.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: The repeated image of sound, straight completion makes the endpoint purposeful evenness rather than accidental debris.

## moral field externalized
- reading: The focus is the communal exterior of the earlier inner mechanism: concealed corruption becomes a cut against life, and the damaged moral field becomes a leveled social field.
- mechanism: The self is presented as a cultivable, bounded field: rupture can breach it, protection can guard it, a cultivator can split it for growth, or corruption can be inserted and hidden in it. The focus externalizes those alternatives. Denial hides corruption; hamstringing cuts a growth source; eradication and leveling make the inwardly chosen treatment of the self into a communal landscape.
- trace:
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B001: A wide breach with bursting emergence supplies the dangerous opening of a moral boundary.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B002: Placing oneself inside protection supplies the counter-operation that preserves the boundary.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The farmer who cleaves earth turns success into skilled opening for cultivation rather than uncontrolled rupture.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase supply the result of rightly cultivating the self.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Secret insertion supplies the opposed act of burying or concealing what enters the self.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B003: Corruption and leading astray specify the destructive content of that concealed insertion.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B007: Cutting a source so growth cannot return turns the animal attack into failed cultivation at the social scale.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B001: Culpability binds the external catastrophe to the community's prior treatment of its moral field.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication is the collective-scale manifestation of corruption carried to completion.

## distributed agency
- reading: The focus assigns corporate agency: a collective verdict and excess mobilize one executor, while leaders and followers share the deed's culpable network and therefore its leveled outcome.
- mechanism: Collective denial and overflowing excess generate pressure; one especially wretched member is stirred from rest into action, but the focus returns to plural agency. The mechanism distributes responsibility across instigator, executor, and follower-network, explaining why one agent's deed yields equalized collective consequence.
- trace:
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Collective attribution of falsehood supplies the shared social verdict preceding the singled-out actor.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Water rising into a sweeping force models collective excess as pressure that exceeds its boundary.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B008: The non-dominant mapped image of a people's raised voice gives that pressure a communal, audible carrier.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Stirring something still from rest supplies the transition from collective condition to an individual's mobilization.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness opposed to flourishing marks the selected agent as the concentrated extreme of the group's condition.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: The concrete disabling act is grammatically returned to the plural, making execution socially attributable.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B003: Followers imagined as tails expose a hierarchy in which subordinate adherents remain attached to the leading deed.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization supplies the collective endpoint across differentiated roles in the offense.

## covenanted water share
- reading: They repudiate a publicly communicated covenant over a vulnerable claimant and its water allocation; the physical attack enforces that seizure, and their punishment arrives as the share they assigned themselves.
- mechanism: The messenger's utterance identifies a divinely protected creature and a defined watering portion. Focus branches for covenant and allotted share then recode denial as repudiation of communicated resource law: disabling the animal extinguishes a claimant and seizes an allocation, so the offenders receive an allotted consequence.
- trace:
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B014: A thing's saying as its indication lets the warning make the protected arrangement legible, not merely audible.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply authorized transmission of the arrangement.
  - 91:13 **ٱللَّهِ ٱللَّهِ** ء ل ه B001: Worship and the worshipped one make the creature and its allocation protected by more than local ownership.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The she-camel in its concrete form fixes the claimant later disabled by the focus verb.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A watering portion and its channel turn the warning into a rule governing access to a shared resource.
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B002: Declaring the speaker or report false becomes repudiation of the authorized allocation.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B011: A covenant or pact supplies a binding social form for the communicated protection.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Cutting the animal down is the material means by which its claim on the allocation is cancelled.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B006: An allotted portion, including a portion of punishment, mirrors the seized watering share as the offenders' returned share.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Total eradication gives the returned allotment its collective and terminal scale.

## tail following aftermath
- reading: Crime and aftermath form one tracked motion: the deed grows a tail, each causal clause follows it, and eradication is the trace it finally leaves.
- mechanism: Early following and late succession bracket the focus. Within that frame, the offense's tail image and the repeated causal conjunctions make each event follow in the track of the prior one; the aftermath is not detachable from the initiating deed but is its rearward continuation and residue.
- trace:
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following in sequence establishes an early image of one element tracing the course of another.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B002: A tail or rear end makes culpability the attached trailing part of the act.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication is the event reached when the trailing chain is allowed to run to completion.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Completion and straightness seal the terminal link of the sequence.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Succession and one thing coming behind another extend the following image beyond the focus.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Remainder and trace identify aftermath as what the act leaves behind.

## no rebound closure
- reading: The focus's leveling is juridical closure as well as destruction: no rival, cost, or surviving remainder can reverse the sovereign settlement.
- mechanism: The immediately following negation joins expected harm and diminution to outcome and aftermath. Read back into the focus, sovereign eradication and equalization admit no counter-cost, retaliation, or surviving claimant capable of reopening the settled event.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Fear as expectation of harm supplies the possibility of a future counter-consequence.
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B004: Diminution taking from a thing recasts fear as possible loss to the actor, a loss the clause negates.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The last part and outcome identify what might otherwise rebound upon the one acting.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Sovereign authority supplies the actor whose judgment cannot be checked by a rival claimant.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Complete eradication removes the social power from which a retaliatory aftermath could arise.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization closes the field and leaves no privileged remainder from which the action could be contested.

## enveloping overlay
- reading: A total cover bears down upon the group and resolves into a smoothed plane, making the punishment spatially enveloping as well as terminal.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B002: A covering that encompasses everything supplies the shape of an event enveloping a collective.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B004: What is above and overshadows supplies the vertical orientation activated by the focus's upon-construction.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Total eradication gives the imagined covering destructive completeness.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Wide smooth ground supplies the visual residue after the encompassing force passes.

## generation cut
- reading: Their blow symbolically cuts a protected line of life and succession; collective eradication returns that interruption of futurity upon the society that enacted it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B004: Barrenness and stopped generation make the wound echo as an assault on continuation.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B005: A final rare thing without successor sharpens the image of a future deliberately cut off.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B005: The non-dominant nurture-and-growth image supplies the answering polarity of cultivated continuation.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase establish continuation as a valued process in the preceding moral frame.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The concrete she-camel prevents the generational resonance from floating free of the focus's physical victim.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Eradication mirrors the offenders' local cutting of continuation at the scale of their whole social body.

## hydraulic allotment
- reading: The focus retains a submerged hydraulic logic: boundary-breaking seizure of a water share sends a filled consequence down the social watercourse and leaves the field level.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B002: Overflowing water and sweeping force cast transgression as a resource exceeding its proper boundary.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A defined watering portion and channel provide the concrete hydrological order that is violated.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B004: Watercourse tails and valley ends let consequence run back along the same material network as the violated share.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B007: A large nearly full bucket gives the returned consequence the image of a filled measure.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B009: Smooth open ground supplies the landscape left after the resource violation's consequence has traversed the field.

## driven single track
- reading: The motion they stop is kinetically reversed: the perpetrators are driven in one consequence-track toward the terminal eradication they set in motion.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:14 **فَكَذَّبُوهُ** ك ذ ب B007: A wild animal running a stage and then stopping supplies the first kinetic interruption.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B003: Disabling the back or movement turns interruption into immobilization.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B001: The non-dominant mapped image of driving and expelling reverses the halted animal's motion onto the offenders as forced movement.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B012: Following in a single track makes that reversal a tightly directed sequence rather than diffuse association.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Complete eradication is the terminus toward which the reversed motion drives the offenders.


# Focus 91:15

## anticipated aftermath
- reading: The subject does not anticipate harm from the event's eventual aftermath.
- mechanism: Fear is prospective anticipation, while the object names what an unspecified feminine event eventually leaves or produces.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipated harm makes the negation prospective rather than merely describing calm after an event.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B006: The terminal result of an act supplies the feared object as its eventual aftermath.

## no punitive requital
- reading: The subject is undeterred by, or unexposed to, punitive requital after the deed.
- mechanism: The outcome is not neutral futurity but adverse requital arriving after a deed, so the denial concerns deterrence or exposure to punishment.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Expectation of harm supplies the deterrent force that the negation removes.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B007: Punitive consequence after an offense turns a generic result into retaliatory requital.

## no follow up challenge
- reading: No later claimant, reviewer, or reverser is apprehended after the act.
- mechanism: What comes afterward can be a claimant, review, or attempt at reversal; negated fear then describes an act performed without apprehension of later challenge.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Prospective apprehension supplies the subject's possible exposure to a later challenger.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B008: Following up to question, claim, or reverse supplies an institutional rather than merely emotional aftermath.

## no visible fear residue
- reading: The event's wake leaves no visible residue of fear on the subject.
- mechanism: Fear can manifest on a person, and an aftermath can be a residue; their pairing allows the clause to deny that the event leaves a legible mark of fear on the subject.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B005: Visible manifestation shifts fear from an inner state to an outwardly readable sign.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remaining residue supplies the wake in which such a sign could persist.

## cyclic next turn
- reading: The subject does not apprehend the next turn in an ordered succession of states.
- mechanism: Following, disclosure, and covering make the early context a sequence of alternating states. That sequence revises the aftermath from a single terminal result into the next turn that follows the present one.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Prospective harm supplies the possible anxiety directed toward the next phase.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B005: Coming after in succession turns the aftermath into a successor phase.
  - 91:2 **تَلَىٰهَا** ت ل و B001: Following and sequence establish an explicit context model of one state arriving behind another.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Disclosure supplies one phase of the alternating mechanism.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A covering that rises over its object supplies the contrary phase that follows disclosure.

## revealed or covered fear trace
- reading: Even when aftermath makes states legible, no fear-mark appears or remains on the subject.
- mechanism: The paired operations of making visible and covering over strengthen the focus-only trace model: the question is whether an aftermath exposes a fear-mark or leaves it concealed.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B005: Fear appearing on a person provides the potentially visible mark.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Residual trace provides the temporal-material location where the mark would remain.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Uncovering and appearance activate the possibility that an inner state becomes legible.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: Covering supplies the rival operation that can hide a trace without erasing it.

## formation leveling reversal
- reading: The agent of leveling does not fear the aftermath of reversing formed distinctions into an equalized field.
- mechanism: Composition and the first fashioning establish differentiated form; the repeated fashioning root after extirpation can instead equalize. The focus aftermath then becomes a reversal that turns formed distinctions back into a level condition.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipation of harm supplies the possible cost of reversing an earlier formation.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B003: Turning back on the heel supplies the reversal geometry within the aftermath.
  - 91:5 **بَنَىٰهَا** ب ن ي B001: Joining parts into a construction supplies the initial production of ordered form.
  - 91:7 **سَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B002: Integrity and completion supply the first occurrence as fashioning a whole self.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Making things equal supplies the second occurrence as leveling distinctions.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation makes the later equalization materially destructive rather than merely formative.

## protection versus fear
- reading: The subject does not occupy the vulnerable position that must shield itself from later backlash, whether through recklessness or immunity.
- mechanism: A breached limit and active protection are paired in the moral context. Their contrast revises fear from bare emotion into awareness of exposure and the need to shield oneself from what follows.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Expected harm provides the exposure against which protection would normally respond.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B007: Punitive consequence specifies the backlash from which an exposed actor might seek protection.
  - 91:8 **فُجُورَهَا** ف ج ر B004: Deviation that tears a protective boundary supplies the production of exposure.
  - 91:8 **وَتَقْوَىٰهَا** و ق ي B001: Warding off harm supplies the active precaution that is distinct from merely feeling fear.

## growth to withering aftermath
- reading: The aftermath can be pictured as the withering phase that follows cultivated growth or its burial.
- mechanism: Plowing, growth, and concealment activate the focus root's plant-stage branch. The aftermath becomes not an abstract verdict but a terminal material phase in which cultivated or buried growth yellows and dries.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipated harm allows a terminal material stage to function as the feared prospect.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B015: Plant yellowing toward dryness supplies a concrete terminal stage for the aftermath.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: The plowman cutting the earth supplies cultivation as the beginning of the material sequence.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth and increase provide the flourishing middle phase.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Hidden insertion supplies burial or suppression before the terminal stage.

## reckless agent no deterrence
- reading: The most wretched perpetrator surges into the deed without fear of its punitive aftermath.
- mechanism: Concealment, boundary-crossing, sudden self-rousing, and the singular wretched agent form an alternative subject chain. On this reading, the final singular describes the perpetrator's reckless refusal to be deterred by requital.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Prospective harm supplies the deterrence that the reckless agent lacks.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B007: Punitive consequence supplies what should have checked the perpetrator's action.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Concealment supplies the suppression of what might otherwise remain morally visible.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Crossing the limit in disobedience supplies the agent's transgressive momentum.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B001: Rousing what was still supplies the abrupt launch into action.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B001: Wretchedness supplies the singular candidate actor whose conduct remains undeterred.

## offense to requital chain
- reading: The aftermath is the requital generated by a warned, boundary-crossing offense and its violent execution.
- mechanism: Denial and limit-crossing confront a delivered message, proceed to crippling violence, and are followed by extirpation explicitly tied to offense. This sequence turns the baseline punitive branch into a concrete offense-to-requital chain.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipated harm supplies fear's deterrent orientation toward requital.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B007: Punishment after wrongdoing supplies the legal-causal endpoint of the chain.
  - 91:11 **كَذَّبَتْ** ك ذ ب B002: Attributing falsehood supplies deliberate rejection as the first actionable breach.
  - 91:11 **بِطَغْوَىٰهَآ** ط غ ي B001: Transgressing a limit supplies the offense's excess beyond mere disagreement.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: Messenger and message supply prior notice against which rejection and violation become legible.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Crippling an animal at its legs supplies the concrete violent deed.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the comprehensive adverse response.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B001: Offense supplies the stated basis that links the violent act to the response.

## water share retained liability
- reading: The aftermath is an outstanding claim attached to a violated watering allocation, persisting after the protected animal is destroyed.
- mechanism: A messenger's spoken designation joins a protected animal to an allocated watering share; the subsequent crippling violates that allocation. The focus outcome branch then activates as a claim or liability retained after the protected object is destroyed.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipated harm supplies apprehension of an outstanding claim.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B010: Substitution, retained property, and follow-up liability supply an obligation that survives the transaction or loss.
  - 91:13 **فَقَالَ** ق و ل B016: A saying that sets a thing's limit supplies the verbal definition of the protected arrangement.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B002: The messenger and message supply authorized notice of the arrangement.
  - 91:13 **نَاقَةَ** ن و ق B002: The concrete she-camel supplies the protected object to which the allocation attaches.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B003: A share of watering and its channel supply the allocated resource-right.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B002: Crippling the animal supplies the act that destroys the protected arrangement but leaves liability behind.

## sovereign act beyond review
- reading: The sovereign agent's leveling is beyond appellate reversal, counterclaim, or retaliatory exposure.
- mechanism: The nearest singular lord is associated with sovereign mastery, extirpation, and comprehensive equalization. This context revises negated fear into the absence of any superior claimant able to review, reverse, or retaliate against the leveling act.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipation of harm supplies the exposure to reprisal that sovereignty negates.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B008: A follower who reviews, challenges, or reverses supplies the absent appellate power.
  - 91:14 **فَسَوَّىٰهَا** س و ي B001: Equalization supplies the scope of the sovereign act under possible review.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the act's comprehensive and irreversible force.
  - 91:14 **رَبُّهُم** ر ب ب B001: Mastery and sovereignty supply the nearest singular agent and the lack of a superior authority.

## no surviving tail
- reading: The subject does not fear a surviving descendant, follower, or remnant coming after the destroyed body.
- mechanism: Interrupted posterity, extirpation, and the image of a tail or followers cluster around the final action. They activate the focus aftermath as a surviving continuation capable of answering back; the feared follow-on is absent because the chain has been cut.
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipated harm supplies the possibility that survivors could retaliate or renew the conflict.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B004: Posterity left after a person supplies the possible surviving continuation.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: A remainder or residual trace broadens posterity into any surviving after-part.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B004: Interruption of offspring supplies a severed generational line.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the removal of a collective remainder.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B003: Tails and followers supply the social body that would otherwise continue behind the destroyed group.

## ecological withering
- reading: The aftermath briefly appears as the drying terminal phase of something cultivated, grown, and then buried.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B015: Yellowing toward dryness supplies the surprising terminal image.
  - 91:9 **أَفْلَحَ** ف ل ح B003: Plowing the earth supplies the start of a cultivation sequence.
  - 91:9 **زَكَّىٰهَا** ز ك و B001: Growth supplies the phase whose later decline becomes meaningful.
  - 91:10 **دَسَّىٰهَا** د س و B001: Concealed insertion supplies burial or suppression in the material analogy.

## pursuer on the heels
- reading: The subject fears no pursuer or claimant dispatched to come immediately on the act's heels.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Expected harm supplies apprehension of a pursuer coming from behind.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B002: Heel and rear trace spatialize the aftermath as movement immediately behind.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B008: A pursuing claimant supplies the agent who follows the trace.
  - 91:12 **ٱنۢبَعَثَ** ب ع ث B002: Dispatch and direction supply the launching of a moving agent.
  - 91:13 **رَسُولُ** ر س ل B001: Sending and setting in motion reinforce the dispatched-agent pattern.
  - 91:13 **وَسُقْيَٰهَا** س ق ي B012: The non-dominant mapped branch of following one trace supplies the kinetic bridge to pursuit.

## severed posterity
- reading: The aftermath is who remains later: descendants, followers, or residue capable of answering the act.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B004: Posterity supplies descendants as a living aftermath.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Residue supplies non-genealogical remnants alongside descendants.
  - 91:14 **فَعَقَرُوهَا** ع ق ر B004: Interrupted offspring supplies the cutting of posterity.
  - 91:14 **فَدَمْدَمَ** د م د م B001: Extirpation supplies the removal of a remainder at collective scale.
  - 91:14 **بِذَنۢبِهِمْ** ذ ن ب B003: Followers imagined as tails supply the social continuation behind a body.

## steep pass of hardship
- reading: The consequence is also spatially ahead: a steep passage whose difficulty does not deter the subject.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B001: Anticipated harm supplies apprehension before entering difficult terrain.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B012: A steep and jutting pass supplies the spatial image of a hard aftermath.
  - 91:12 **أَشْقَىٰهَا** ش ق و B004: A long upward mountain flank supplies the context bridge from hardship to ascent.

## unreadable fear mark
- reading: Fear would be a mark exposed in the wake, yet the subject's aftermath carries no readable sign of it.
- mechanism: 
- trace:
  - 91:15 **يَخَافُ** خ و ف B005: Fear appearing on a person supplies an outward diagnostic sign.
  - 91:15 **عُقْبَٰهَا** ع ق ب B011: Residue supplies the enduring trace in which that sign could be read.
  - 91:3 **جَلَّىٰهَا** ج ل و B001: Revelation and appearance supply the act of making the sign visible.
  - 91:4 **يَغْشَىٰهَا** غ ش و B001: A concealing cover supplies the contrary possibility of obscuring the sign.
  - 91:5 **وَٱلسَّمَآءِ** س م و B001: The non-dominant mapped image of a visible identifying mark sharpens the aftermath as legible evidence.
